|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

mauggal‍lānabyākaraṇaṃ

1. paṭhamau sañ‍ñādikaṇḍau

siddhamiddhaguṇaṃ sādhu, namassitvā tathāgataṃ|

sadhammasaṅghaṃ bhāsissaṃ, māgadhaṃ saddalakkhaṇaṃ||

1. aādayau  titālīsa vaṇṇā|

akārādayau niggahītantā taicattālīsa-kkharā vaṇṇā nāma haunti| a ā i ī u ū ai ai au au, ka kha ga gha ṅa ca cha ja jha ñ‍ña, ṭa ṭha ḍa ḍha ṇa, ta tha da dha na, pa pha ba bha ma, ya ra la va sa ha ḷa aṃ| taina k‍vatthau? ‘‘ai au na mavaṇṇai’’ 1. 37| titālīsābhi vacanaṃ katthaci vaṇṇalaupaṃ ñāpaiti| taina ‘paṭisaṅkhā yaunisau’tiādi siddhaṃ|

2. dasādau sarā|

tatthādimhi dasa vaṇṇā sarā nāma haunti| taina k‍vatthau? ‘‘sarau laupau sarai’’ 1, 26 ic‍cādi|

3. dvaidvai savaṇṇā| taisu dvaidvai sarā savaṇṇā nāma haunti| taina k‍vatthau? ‘‘vaṇṇaparaina savaṇṇaupi’’ 1, 24|

4. pubbau rassau|

taisu  dvīsu yau yau pubbau, sau sau rassasañ‍ñau hauti| taisu ai.au.saṃyaugatau pubbāva dissanti| taina k‍vatthau? ‘‘rassau vā’’ 2,62 ic‍cādi|

5. parau dīghau|

tai svaiva dvīsuyau yau parau, sau sau dīghasañ‍ñau hauti| taina k‍vatthau? ‘‘yau lau panisu dīghau’’ 2,88 ic‍cādi|

6. kādayau byañ‍janā|

kakārādayau vaṇṇā niggahītapariyantā byañ‍janasañ‍ñāhaunti| taina k‍vatthau? ‘‘byañ‍janai dīgharassā’’ 1,33 ic‍cādi|

7. pañ‍ca pañ‍cakā vaggā|

kādayau pañ‍ca pañ‍cakā vagga, nāma haunti| taina k‍vatthau? ‘‘vaggai vaggantau’’ 1,41 ic‍cādi|

8. bindu niggahītaṃ|

yvāyaṃ vaṇṇau bindumattau, sau niggahītasañ‍ñau hauti| taina k‍vatthau? ‘‘niggahītaṃ’’ 1,38 icādi| garusañ‍ñākaraṇaṃ anvatthasañ‍ñatthaṃ|

9. iyuvaṇṇā  jhalā nāmassantai|

nāmaṃ pāṭipadikaṃ, tassaantai vattamānā ivaṇṇuvaṇṇā jhalasañ‍ñā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ| taina k‍vatthau? ‘‘jhalā vā’’ 2,111 ic‍cādi|

10. pitthiyaṃ|

itthiyaṃ vattamānassa nāmassa-ntai vattamānā ivaṇṇuvaṇṇā pasañ‍ñā haunti| taina k‍vatthau? yai passivaṇṇassa’’ 2,116 ic‍cādi|

11. ghā|

itthiyaṃ vattamānassa nāmassa-ntai vatthamānau ākārau ghasañ‍ñau hauti| taina k‍vatthau? ‘‘ghabrahmāditai’’ 2,60 ic‍cādi|

12. gau syālapanai|

ālapanai si gasañ‍ñau hauti|

taina k‍vatthau? ‘‘gaivā’’ 2,65 ic‍cādi|

(sañ‍ñā)

13. vidhibbisaisanantassa|

yaṃ visaisanaṃ, tadantassa vidhi ñātabbau ‘‘atau yaunaṃ ṭāṭai’’ 2,41 narānarai|

14. sattamiyaṃ pubbassa|

sattamīniddaisai  pubbassaiva kāriyaṃ ñātabbaṃ ‘‘sarau laupau sarai’’ 1,26 vaiḷaggaṃ| ‘tamaha’ntīdha kasmā na hauti?, saraitaupasilaisikādhārau tatthaitāva vuc‍catai ‘pubbassaiva hauti na parassā’ti|

15. pañ‍camiyaṃ parassa|

pañ‍camīniddaisai parassa kāriyaṃ ñātabbaṃ ‘‘atauyaunaṃ ṭāṭai’’ 2,41 narānarai| idha na hautaṃ ‘jantuhau anattā’| idha kasmā na hauti? ausakhyau, anantarai katatthatāya na byavahitassa kāriyaṃ|

16. ādissa|

parassa ssissamānaṃ kāriyamādivaṇṇassa ñātabbaṃ ‘‘ra saṅkhyātau vā’’ 3,103 tairasa|

17. chaṭṭhiyantassa|

chaṭṭhīniddiṭṭhassa yaṃ kāriyaṃ, tadantassa vaṇṇassa viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ ‘‘rājassi nāmhi’’ 2,23 rājinā|

18. ṅānubandhau|

ṅakārau anubandhau yassa, sau antassa hauti ‘‘gaussā vaṅa’’ 1,32 gavāssaṃ|

19. ṭānubandhānaikavaṇṇā sabbassa|

ṭakārau-nubandhau  yassa, sau-naikakkharau cādaisau sabbassa hauti ‘‘imassānitthiyaṃ ṭai’’ 2,128 aisu, ‘‘nāmha-nimi’’ 2,126 anaina|

20. ñakānubandhādyantā|

chaṭṭhīniddiṭṭhassa ñānubandhakānubandhā ādyantā haunti ‘‘brūtau tissīña’’ 6,36 bravīti, ‘‘bhūssa vuka’’ 6,17 babhūva|

21. mānubandhau sarānamantā parau|

makārau-nubandhau yassa, sau sarā namantā sarā parau hauti ‘‘mañ‍ca rudhādīnaṃ’’ 5-19 rundhati|

22. vippaṭisaidhai|

dvin‍naṃ sāvakāsānamaikatthappasaṅgai parau hauti| yathā dvin‍naṃ tiṇṇaṃ vāpurisānaṃ sahappattiyaṃ parau, sau ca (gacchati) tvaṃ ca (gacchasi, tumhai) gacchatha| sau ca (gacchati,) tvaṃ ca (gacchasi,) ahaṃ ca (gacchāmi, mayaṃ) gacchāma|

23. saṅkaitau-navayavau-nubandhau|

yau navayavabhūtausaṅkaitau, sau-nubandhauti ñātabbau, ‘‘lupitādīnamāsimhi’’ 2-57 kattā|

saṅkaitaggahaṇaṃ kiṃ? pakatiyādisamudāyassānubandhatā mā hautūti, anavayavauhi samudāyau… samudāyarūpattāyaiva|

anavayavaggahaṇaṃ kiṃ? ‘‘ataina’’ 2-108 janaina| imināva laupassāvagatattā nānubandhalaupāya vacanamāraddhaṃ|

24. vaṇṇaparaina savaṇṇaupi|

vaṇṇasaddau  parau yasmā taina savaṇṇaupi gayhati saṃca rūpaṃ ‘‘yuvaṇṇānamaiau luttā’’ 1-29 vākairitaṃ, samaunā|

25. ntu vantumantvāvantutavantusambandhī|

vantvādisambandhīyaivantu gayhati, ‘‘ntantūnaṃ ntau yaumhi paṭhamai’’ 2-215 guṇavantau|

vantvādisambandhīti kiṃ? jantū tantū|

(paribhāsāyau|)

26. sarau laupau sarai|

sarai sarau laupanīyau hauti| tatri-mai, saddhi-ndriyaṃ, nauhai-taṃ, bhikkhunau-vādau, samaitā-yasmā, abhibhā-yatanaṃ, puttāma-tthi, asantai-ttha|

27. parau k‍vaci|

saramhā parau sarau k‍vaci laupanīyau hauti| sau-pi, sāva, yatau-dakaṃ, tatau-va| k‍vacītikiṃ? saddhi-ndriyaṃ, ayamadhikārau āparicchaidāvasānā, taina nātippasaṅgau|

28. na dvaivā|

pubbaparasarā dvaipi vā k‍vaci na lupyantai, latā iva, latai-va, latā-va|

29. yuvaṇṇānamaiau luttā|

luttā  sarā paraisaṃ ivaṇṇuvaṇṇānaṃ aiau haunti vā yathāk‍kamaṃ|

tassai-daṃ, vātai-ritaṃ, nau-paiti, vāmau-rū, atai-vañ‍ñaihi, vau-dakaṃ| kathaṃ ‘‘pac‍caurasmi’’nti? yaugavibhāgā| vātvaiva? tassidaṃ|

luttaiti kiṃ? latā iva|

30. yavā sarai|

sarai parai ivaṇṇuvaṇṇānaṃ yakāravakārā haunti vā yathāk‍kamaṃ| byākatau, ic‍cassa, ajjhiṇamuttau, svāgataṃ, svāpanalānilaṃ, vātvaiva? iti-ssa| k‍vacitvaiva? yānī-dha, sū-paṭṭhitaṃ|

31. aiaunaṃ|

aiaunaṃyavāyaunti vā sarai yathāk‍kamaṃ| tyaj‍ja tai-j‍ja, svāhaṃ sau-haṃ| k‍vacitvaiva? puttāma-tthi, asantai-ttha|

32. gaussā-vaṅa|

sarai gaussa avaṅa hauti| gavā-ssaṃ| ‘yatharīva, tatharivai’ti nipātāva, ‘bhusāmivai’ti ivasaddau aivatthau|

33. byañ‍janai dīgharassā|

rassadīghānaṃ  k‍vaci dīgharassā haunti byañ‍janai| tatrā-yaṃ, munīcarai, sammadaiva, mālabhārī|

34. saramhā dvai|

saramhā parassa byañ‍janassa k‍vaci dvai rūpāni haunti| paggahau| saramhāti kiṃ? taṃ khaṇaṃ|

35. catutthadutiyaisvaisaṃ tatiyapaṭhamā|

catutthadutiyaisu paraisvaisaṃ catutthadutiyānaṃ tabbaggai tatiya paṭhamā haunti| pac‍cāsatthyā, nigghausau, akkhanti, baujjhaṅgā, saitacchattaṃ, daḍḍhau, niṭṭhānaṃ, mahaddhanau, yasatthairau, apphuṭaṃ, abbhuggatau| aisvīti kiṃ? thairau| aisanti kiṃ? patthau|

36. vitissaivai vā|

aivasaddai parai itissa vau hauti vā| itvaiva, ic‍caiva| aivaiti kiṃ? ic‍cāha|

37. aiaunama vaṇṇai|

aiaunaṃ vaṇṇai k‍vaci a hauti vā| disvā yācaka māgatai, akaramhasa tai, aisa atthau, aisa dhammau, aggamakkhāyati, svātanaṃ, hiyyattanaṃ, karassu| vātvaiva? yācakai āgatai aisau dhammau| vaṇṇaiti kiṃ? sau|

38. niggahītaṃ|

niggahītamāgamau hauti vā k‍vaci| cakkhuṃ udapādi cakkhuudapādi, purimaṃ jātiṃ purimajātiṃ, kattabbaṃ kusalaṃ bahuṃ| avaṃsirautiādīsu nic‍caṃ… vavatthitavibhāsattā vādhikārassa, sāmattiyaināgamauva, sa ca rassa sarassaiva hauti… tassa rassānugatattā|

39. laupau|

niggahītassa laupau hauti vā k‍vaci| kyāhaṃ kimahaṃsārattau saṃrattau| sal‍lai khau-gantukāmau gantumanauti, ādīsu nic‍caṃ|

40. parasarassa|

niggahītamhā parassa sarassa laupau hauti vā k‍vaci| tvaṃ-si tvamasi|

41. vaggai vaggantau|

niggahītassa khau vaggai vaggantau vā hauti pac‍cāsattyā| taṅkarauti taṃ karauti, tañ‍carati taṃ carati, taṇṭhānaṃ taṃ ṭhānaṃ, tandhanaṃ taṃ dhanaṃ, tampāti taṃ pāti| nic‍caṃ padamajjhai gantvā, k‍vacañ‍ñātrapi santiṭṭhati|

42. yaivahisu ñau|

ya  aiva hi saddaisu niggahītassa vā ñau hauti| yañ‍ñadaiva, tañ‍ñaiva, tañhi, vātvaiva? yaṃ yadaiva|

43. yai saṃssa|

saṃsaddassa yaṃ niggahītaṃ tassa vā ñau hauti yakārai| sañ‍ñamau saṃyamau|

44. mayadā sarai|

niggahītassa mayadā haunti vā sarai k‍vaci| tamahaṃ, tayidaṃ, tadalaṃ| vā tvaiva? taṃ ahaṃ|

45. vanataragā cāgamā|

aitai mayadā ca āgamā haunti sarai vā k‍vaci| tivaṅgikaṃ, itau nāyati, cinitvā, tasmātiha, niraujaṃ, puthagaiva, idhamāhu, yathayidaṃ, attadatthaṃ| vā tvaiva? attatthaṃ| ‘atippagau khau tāvā’ti-paṭhamantau pagasaddauva|

46. chā ḷau|

chasaddā parassa sarassa ḷakārau āgamau hauti vā| chaḷaṅgaṃ, chaḷāyatanaṃ| vātvaiva? chaabhiñ‍ñā|

47. tadaminādīni|

tadaminādīni  sādhūni bhavanti| taṃ iminā tadaminā, sakiṃ āgāmī sakadāgāmī, aikaṃ idha ahaṃ aikamidāhaṃ, saṃvidhāya avahārau saṃvidāvahārau, vārinau vāhakau valāhakau, jīvanassa mūtau jīmūtau, chavassa sayanaṃ susānaṃ, uddhaṃ khamassa udukkhalaṃ, pisitāsau pisācau, mahiyaṃ ravatīti mayūrau, aivamañ‍ñaipi payaugatau-nugantabbā, paraisaṃ pisaudarādimivaidaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ|

48. tavaggavaraṇānaṃ yai cavaggabayañā|

tavaggavaraṇānaṃ k‍vaci cavaggabayañā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ yakārai| apuc‍caṇḍatāya, tacchaṃ, yaj‍jaivaṃ, ajjhattaṃ, thañ‍ñaṃ, dibbaṃ, payyausanā, paukkharañ‍ñau| k‍vacitvaiva? rattyā

49. vaggalasaihi tai|

vaggalasaihi parassa yakārassa k‍vaci tai vaggalasā haunti|

sak‍katai, pac‍catai, aṭṭatai, kuppatai, phal‍latai, assatai| k‍vacitvaiva? kyāhaṃ|

50. hassa vipal‍lāsau|

hassa vipal‍lāsau hauti yakārai| guyhaṃ|

51. vai vā|

hassa vipal‍lāsau hauti vā vakārai| bavhābādhau bavhābādhau|

52. tathānarānaṃ ṭaṭhaṇalā

tathanarānaṃ  ṭaṭhaṇalā haunti vā| duk‍kaṭaṃ, aṭṭhakathā, gahaṇaṃ, palighau, palāyati| vātvaiva? duk‍kataṃ| k‍vacitvaiva? sugatau|

53. saṃyaugādi laupau|

saṃyaugassa yau ādībhūtau-vayavau tassa vā k‍vaci laupau hauti| pupphaṃ-sā jāyatai-gini|

54. vicchābhikkhañ‍ñaisu dvai|

vicchāyamābhikkhañ‍ñai ca yaṃ vattatai, tassa dvai rūpāni haunti| kriyāya guṇaina dabbaina vā bhin‍nai atthai byāpitumicchā vicchā| rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ siñ‍cati, gāmau gāmau ramaṇīyau, gāmai gāmai pānīyaṃ, gaihai gaihai issarau, rasaṃ rasaṃ bhakkhayati, kiriyaṃ kiriyamārabhatai|

atthiyaivā-nupubbiyaipi vicchā mūlai mūlai thūlā, aggai aggai sukhumā, yadi hi aittha mūlaggabhaidau na siyā, ānupubbiyampi na bhavaiyya| māsakaṃ māsakaṃ imamhā kahāpaṇā bhavanthānaṃ dvin‍naṃ daihīti māsakaṃ māsakamic‍caitasmā vicchāgamyatai, saddantaratau pana imamhā kahāpaṇāti avadhāraṇaṃ| pubbaṃ pubbaṃ pupphanti, paṭhamaṃ paṭhamaṃ pac‍cantītyatrāpi vicchāva| imai ubhau aḍḍhā katarā katarā aisaṃ dvin‍namaḍḍhatā, sabbai imai aḍḍhā katamā katamā imaisaṃ aḍḍhutā ihāpi  vicchāva| ābhikkhañ‍ñaṃ paunaupuñ‍ñaṃ pacati pacati, papacati papacati, lunāhi lunāhitvaivāyaṃ lunāti, bhutvā bhutvā gacchati, paṭapaṭā karauti, paṭapaṭāyati|

55. syādilaupau pubbassaikassa|

vicchāyamaikassa dvittai pubbassa syādilaupau hauti| aikaikassa| kathaṃ matthakamatthakaināti? ‘syādilaupau pubbassā’ti yaugavibhāgā, nācātippasaṅgau yaugavibhāgā iṭṭhappasiddhīti|

56. sabbādīnaṃ vītihārai|

sabbādīnaṃ vītihārai dvai bhavanti pubbassa syādilaupau ca| añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa bhaujakā, itarītarassa bhaujakā|

57. yāvabaudhaṃ sambhamai|

turitaināpāyahaitupadassanaṃ sambhamau, tasmiṃsati vatthu yāvantaihi saddaihi sau-tthau viñ‍ñāyatai, tāvantau saddā payuj‍jantai| sappau sappau sappau, bujjhassu bujjhassu bujjhassu, bhin‍nau bhikkhusaṅghau bhin‍nau bhikkhusaṅghau|

58. bahulaṃ|

ayamadhikārau āsatthaparisamattiyā| taina nātippasaṅgau iṭṭhappasiddhi ca|

iti mauggal‍lānai byākaraṇai vuttiyaṃ

paṭhamau kaṇḍau|

2. dutiyau kaṇḍau (syādi)

1. dvai  dvai-kānaikaisu nāmasmā si yau, aṃ yau, nā hi, sa naṃ, smā hi, sa naṃ, smiṃ su|

aitaisaṃ dvai dvai haunti aikānaikatthaisu vattamānatau nāmasmā| muni munayau, muniṃ munayau, muninā munīhi, munissa munīnaṃ, munismā munīhi, munissa munīnaṃ, munismiṃ munīsu, aivaṃ kumārī kumāriyau, kañ‍ñā kañ‍ñāyauti| aitāni satta dukāni satta vibhattiyau vibhāgau vibhatīti katvā, aittha siamitī-kārā-kārā ‘‘kimaṃ sisu’’ 2,200 ti saṃkaitatthā|

2. kammai dutiyā|

karīyati kattu kiriyāyā-bhisambandhīyatīti kammaṃ, tasmiṃ dutiyāvibhatti hautaṃ| kaṭaṃ karauti, audanaṃ pacati, ādic‍caṃ passati|

‘audanau pac‍catī’ti audanasaddatau kammatā nappatīyatai, kiñ‍carahi? ākhyātatau| ‘kaṭaṃ karauti vipulaṃ dassanīya’nti atthaiva guṇayuttassa kammatā, icchitaipi kammattāva dutiyā siddhā gāvuṃ payau dauhati, gaumantaṃ gāvaṃ yācati, gāvamavarundhati vajaṃ, māṇavakaṃ maggaṃ pucchati, gaumantaṃ gāvaṃ bhikkhatai, rukkhamavacināti phalāni, sissaṃ dhammaṃ brūtai, sissaṃ dhammamanusāsatīti| aivaṃ anicchitaipi ahiṃ laṅghayati, visaṃ bhakkhaiti| yaṃnaivicchitaṃ nāpi  anicchitaṃ, tatthāpi dutiyā siddhā| gāmaṃ gacchantau rukkhamūlamupasappati|

pathaviṃ adhisaissati, gāmamadhitiṭṭhati, rukkhamajjhāsataitiadhisīṭhāsānaṃpayaugai-dhikaraṇai kammavacanicchā, vatticchātau hi kārakāni haunti| taṃ yathā-valāhakā vij‍jautatai, valāhakassa vij‍jautatai, valāhakau vij‍jautatai, valāhakai vij‍jautatai, valāhakaina vij‍jautataiti| aivamabhinivisassa vā dhammamabhinivisatai dhammai vā|

tathā upanvajjhāvasassābhaujana nivuttivacanassa gāmamupavasati, ga, mamanuvasati, pabbatamadhivasati, gharamāvasati| abhaujananivutti vacanassāti kiṃ? gāmai upavasati, bhaujananivuttiṃ karautīti atthau| tappānācāraipi kammattāva dutiyā siyā nadimpivati, gāmaṃ carati, aivaṃ ‘sacai maṃ ālapissatī’tiādīsupi| vihitāva patidhayaugai dutiyā – ‘paṭibhantu taṃ cunda baujjhaṅgā’ti| taṃ pati baujjhaṅgā bhāsantūti atthau, yadātu dhātunāyuttau pati, tadā tainā-yaugā sambandhai chaṭṭhīva ‘tassa nappaṭibhātī’ti| akkhai dibbati, akkhaihi dibbati, akkhaisu dibbatīti kammakaraṇādhikaraṇavacanicchā|

3. kāladdhānamac‍cantasaṃyaugai|

kiriyā, guṇa, dabbaihi sākal‍laina kāladdhānaṃ sambandhau ac‍cantasaṃyaugau| tasmiṃ viñ‍ñāyamānai kālasaddaihi addhaaddaihi ca dutiyā hauti| māsamadhītai, māsaṃ kalyāṇi, māsaṃ  guḷadhānā, kausamadhītai, kausaṃ kuṭilā nadī, kausaṃ pabbatau| acantasaṃyaugaiti kiṃ? māsassa dvīhamadhītai, kausassaikadaisai pabbatau|

pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā, aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā, imaṃ rattiṃ cattārau mahārājāti aivamādīsu kālavācī hi ac‍cantasaṃyaugattāva dutiyā siddhā| vibhattivipal‍lāsainapi vā bahulaṃvidhānā|

phalappattiyaṃ kiriyāparisamattyapavaggau, tasmiṃ viñ‍ñāyamānai kāladdhānaṃ kiriyāyāc‍cantasaṃyaugai tatiyābhimatā, sāpi karaṇattāva siddhā ‘māsainānuvākau-dhītau, kausainānuvākau-dhītau’ti| anapavaggaitu asādhakatamattākaraṇattābhāvai dutiyāva ‘māsamadhītau-nuvākau, na cānai na gahitau’ti|

kārakamajjhai yai kāladdhānavācinau, tatau sattamīpañ‍camiyau abhimatā ‘aj‍ja bhutvā daivadattau dvihai bhuñ‍jissati, dvīhā bhuñ‍jissati, atraṭṭhau-yamissāsau kausai lakkhaṃ vijjhati, kausā lakkhaṃ vijjhatī’ti, tāpīha sakasakakārakavacanicchāyaiva siddhā|

4. gati baudhāhāra saddatthākammaka bhaj‍jādīnaṃ payauj‍jai|

gamanatthānaṃ baudhatthānaṃ āhāratthānaṃ saddatthānamakammakānaṃ bhaj‍jādīnañ‍ca payauj‍jai kattari dutiyā hauti| sāmatthiyā ca payaujakabyāpāraina kammatāvassa hautīti patīyatai| gamayati māṇavakaṃ gāmaṃ, yāpayati māṇavakaṃ gāmaṃ, baudhayati māṇavakaṃ dhammaṃ, vaidayati māṇavakaṃ dhammaṃ, bhaujayati māṇa-vakaṃ  maudakaṃ, āsayati māṇavakaṃ maudakaṃ, ajjhāpayati māṇavakaṃ vaidaṃ, pāṭhayati māṇavakaṃ vaidaṃ, āsayati daivadattaṃ, sāyayati daivadattaṃ, añ‍ñaṃ bhaj‍jāpaiti, añ‍ñaṃ kauṭṭāpaiti, añ‍ñaṃ santharāpaiti| aitaisamaivāti kiṃ? pācaiti audanaṃ daivadattaina yañ‍ñadattau| payauj‍jaiti kiṃ? gacchati daivadattau| yadā carahi gamayati daivadattaṃ yañ‍ñadattau, tamaparau payaujaiti, tadā gamayati daivadattaṃ yañ‍ñadattainaiti bhavitabbaṃ … gamayatissā-gamanatthattā|

5. harādīnaṃ vā|

harādīnaṃ payauj‍jai kattari dutiyā hauti vā| hāraiti bhāraṃ daivadattaṃ daivadattainaiti vā, ajjhauhāraiti sattuṃ daivadattaṃ daivadattainaiti vā, kāraiti kaṭaṃ daivadattaṃ daivadattainaiti vā dassayatai janaṃ rājaṃ janainaiti vā, abhivādayatai guruṃ daivadattaṃ daivadattainaiti vā|

6. na khādādīnaṃ|

khādādīnaṃ payauj‍jai kattari dutiyā na hauti| khādayati daivadattaina, ādayati daivadattaina, avha, payati daivadattaina, saddāyayati daivadattaina, kandayati daivadattaina, nāyayati daivadattaina|

(1) vahissāniyantukai| vāhayati bhāraṃ daivadattaina aniyantukaiti kiṃ? vāha-yati bhāraṃ balībaddai|

(2) bhakkhissāhiṃ  sāyaṃ| bhakkhayati maudakai daivadattaina| ahiṃsāyanti kiṃ? bhakkhayati balībaddai sassaṃ|

7. jhādīhi yuttā|

dhīādīhi yuttatau dutiyā hauti| dhiratthu maṃ pūtikāyaṃ, antarā ca rājagahaṃ antarā ca nālandaṃ, samādhānamantaraina, mucalindamabhitau saramic‍cādi, chaṭṭhiyāpavādau-yaṃ|

8. lakkhaṇitthambhūtavicchāsvabhinā|

lakkhaṇādīsvatthaisvabhinā yuttamhā dutiyā mahāti| rukkhamabhi vij‍jautatai vij‍ju, sādhu daivadattau mātaramabhi, rukkhaṃ rukkhamabhitiṭṭhati|

9. patiparīhi bhāgai ca|

patiparīhi yuttamhā lakkhaṇādīsu bhāgai catthai dutiyā hauti| rukkhaṃ pati vij‍jautatai vij‍ju, sādhu daivadattau mātaraṃ pati, rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ pati tiṭṭhati, yadaittha maṃ pati siyā| rukkhaṃ pari vij‍jautatai vij‍ju, sādhu daivadattau mātaraṃ pari, rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ pari tiṭṭhati, yadaittha maṃ pari siyā|

10. anunā|

lakkhaṇādīsvatthaisvanunā yuttamhā dutiyā hauti| rukkhamanu vij‍jautatai vij‍ju, sac‍cakiriyamanu vuṭṭhi pāvassi, haitu ca lakkhaṇaṃ bhavati, sādhu daivadattau mātaramanu, rukkhaṃ rukkhamanu tiṭṭhati, yadaittha maṃ anu siyā|

11. sahatthai|

sahatthai-nunā  yuttamhā dutiyā hauti| pabbatamanu sainā tiṭṭhati|

12. hīnai|

hīnatthai-nunā yuttamhā dutiyā hauti| anu sāriputtaṃ pañ‍ñavantau|

13. upaina|

hīnatthai upaina yuttamhā dutiyā hauti| upa sāriputtaṃ pañ‍ñavanthau|

14. sattamyādhikyai|

ādhikyatthai upaina yuttamhā sattamī hauti| upa khāriyaṃ dauṇau|

15. sāmittai-dhinā|

sāmibhāvatthai-dhinā yuttamhā sattamī hauti| adhi brahmadattai pañ‍cālā, adhi pañ‍cālaisu brahmadattau|

16. kattukaraiṇaisu tatiyā|

kattari karaṇai ca kārakai tatiyā hauti| purisaina kataṃ, asinā chindati| pakatiyā-bhirūpau, gautthaina gautamau, sumaidhau nāma nāmaina, jātiyā sattavassikauti bhūdhātussa sambhavā karaṇai aiva tatiyā| aivaṃ samaina dhāvati visamaina  dhāvati, dvidauṇaina dhañ‍ñaṃ kiṇāti, pañ‍cakaina pasavau kiṇātīti|

17. sahatthaina|

sahatthaina yaugai tatiyā siyā| puttaina saha gatau, puttaina saddhiṃ āgatau, tatiyāpi chaṭṭhīva appadhānai aiva bhavati|

18. lakkhaṇai|

lakkhaṇai vattamānatau tatiyā siyā| tidaṇḍakaina paribbājakamaddakkhī, akkhinā kāṇau, taina hi aṅgaina aṅginau vikārau lakkhīyatai|

19. haitumhi|

tak‍kiriyā yauggai tatiyā siyā| an‍naina vasati, vij‍jāya yasau|

20. pañ‍camīṇai vā|

iṇai haitumhi pañ‍camī hauti vā| satasmā baddhau, sataina vā|

21. guṇai|

paraṅgabhūtai haitumhi pañ‍camī hauti vā| jaḷattā baddhau jaḷattaina vā, pañ‍ñāya muttau, hutvā abhāvatauanic‍cā, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau|

22. chaṭṭhī  haitvatthaihi|

haitvatthavācīhi yaugai haitumhi chaṭṭhī siyā| udarassa haitu, udarassa kāraṇā|

23. sabbāditau sabbā|

haitvatthaihi yaugai sabbādīhi sabbā vibhattiyau haunti| kau haitu, kaṃ haituṃ, kaina haitunā, kassa haitussa, kasmā haitusmā, kassa haitussa, kasmiṃ haitusmiṃ, kiṃ kāraṇaṃ, kaina kāraṇaina, kiṃ nimittaṃ, kaina nimittaina, kiṃ payaujanaṃ, kaina payaujanaina ic‍caivamādi| haitvatthaihītvaiva? kaina kataṃ|

24. catutthī sampadānai|

yassa sammā padīyatai tasmiṃ catutthī siyā| saṅghassa dadāti| ādhāravivakkhāyaṃ sattamīpi siyā saṅghai daihi|

25. tādatthyai|

tassai-daṃ tadatthaṃ, tadatthabhāvai jautanīyai nāmasmā catutthī siyā| sītassa paṭighātāya, atthāya hitāya (sukhāya) daivamanussānaṃ, nālaṃ dārabharaṇāya, yūpāya dāru, pākāya vajatītvaivamādi|

kassa sāduṃ na ruc‍cabhi, mā-yasmantānampi saṅghabhaidau ruc‍cittha, khamati saṅghassa, bhattamassa nacchādaisīti chaṭṭhī sambandhavacanicchāyaṃ, na caivaṃ viraudhau siyā sadisarūpattā, aivaṃvidhaisu ca sambandhassa saddikānumatattā, kassa vā tvaṃ dhammaṃ raucaisīti atthamattai paṭhamā|

aivamañ‍ñāpi vidhañ‍ñayyā, parataupi yathāgamaṃ|

rañ‍ñau sataṃ dhāraiti, rañ‍ñau chattaṃ dhāraitīti sambandhai chaṭṭhī, aivaṃ rañ‍ñau silāghatai, rañ‍ñau hanutai, rañ‍ñau upatiṭṭhatai, rañ‍ñau sapatai, daivāpi tassa pihayanti tādinau, tassa kujjha mahāvīra, yadihaṃ tassa pakuppaiyyaṃ, duhayati disānaṃ maighau, yau mittānaṃ na dūbhati, yau appaduṭṭhassa narassa dussati, kyāhaṃ ayyānaṃ aparajjhāmi, issayanti samaṇānaṃ titthiyā, dhammaina nayamānānaṃ kā usūyā rañ‍ñau bhāgyamārajjhati, rañ‍ñau bhāgyamikkhatai, taina yācitau ayācitau vā tassa gāvau paṭisuṇāti, gāvau āsuṇāti, bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ, hautu patigiṇāti, hautvanugiṇāti, āraucayāmi vau pativaidayāmi vau, dhammaṃ tai daisaissāmi, yathā vau bhagavā byākaraiyya, alaṃ tai idha vāsaina, kiṃ tai jaṭāhi dummaidha, arahati mal‍lau mal‍lassāti|

jīvitaṃ tiṇāyapi na mañ‍ñamānauti tādatthya catutthī, tiṇaina yau atthau tadatthāyapīti atthau, ‘‘yau ca sītañ‍ca uṇhañ‍ca, tiṇā bhiyyau na mañ‍ñati’’ tiṇamiva jīvitaṃ mañ‍ñamānauti savisayāva vibhattiyau| saggāya gacchatīti tādatthyai catutthī, yau hi saggaṃ gacchati tadatthaṃ tassa gamananti, kammavacanicchāyantu dutiyāva saggaṃ gacchatīti|

āyu  bhautau hautu, ciraṃ jīvitaṃ, bhaddaṃ kalyāṇaṃ atthaṃ payaujanaṃ, kusalaṃ anāmayaṃ, hitaṃ pathyaṃ sukhaṃ sātaṃ bhautau hautu, sādhu sammuti maitassa, puttassāvikaraiyya guyhamatthaṃ, tassa mai sak‍kau pāturahausi, tassa pahiṇaiyya, bhikkhūnaṃ dutaṃ pāhaisi, kappati samaṇānaṃ āyaugau, aikassa dvin‍naṃ tiṇṇaṃ vā pahauti, upamaṃ tai karissāmi, añ‍jaliṃ tai paggaṇhāmi, tassa phāsu, laukassatthau, namau tai purisājañ‍ña, sautthi tassa, alaṃ mal‍lau mal‍lassa, samatthau mal‍lau mal‍lassa, tassa hitaṃ, tassa sukhaṃ, svāgataṃ tai mahārājāti sabbattha chaṭṭhī sambandhai, aivaṃvidhamañ‍ñampaiva viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ yathāgamaṃ|

26. pañ‍camyavadhismā|

padatthāvadhismā pañ‍camīvibhatti hauti| gāmasmā āgacchatu, aivaṃ caurasmā bhāyati, caurasmā uttasati, aurasmā tāyati, caurasmā rakkhatīti, sacai bhāyatha dukkhassa, pamādai bhayadassivā, tasanti daṇḍassāti chaṭṭhī sattamiyaupi hauntaiva sambandhādhāravacanicchāyaṃ|

ajjhainā parājaiti, paṭipakkhai parājaitīti savisayāva vibhattiyau| sacai kaivaṭṭassa paraj‍jissāmīti chaṭṭhīpi hauti sambandhavacanicchāyaṃ| yavaihi gāvau vāraiti, pāpā cittaṃ nivārayai, kākai rakkhati taṇḍulāti savisayaiva pañ‍camī| cittaṃ rakkhaitha maidhāvīti dutiyāva dissati kammatthai| upajjhāyā antaradhāyati, upajjhāyā adhitai, kāmatau jāyatai saukauti savisayai pañ‍camī|

tatthaiva-ntaradhāyi su, naṭassa suṇauti, padumaṃ tattha jāyaithāti sattamīchaṭṭhiyaupi hauntaiva savisayai| himavantā pabhavati gaṅgā, pāṇātipātā viramassu khippaṃ, añ‍ñau daivadattā, bhin‍nau daivadattāti savisayaiva pañ‍camī| aivaṃ ārā sau āsavakkhayā, itarau daivadattā, uddhaṃ pādatalā adhau kaisamatthakā, pubbau gāmā, pubbaiva sambaudhā, tatau paraṃ, tatau aparaina samayaina, tatuttarinti| sambandhavacanicchāyaṃ chaṭṭhīpi puratau gāmassa, dakkhiṇatau gāmassa, upari pabbatassa, haiṭṭhā pāsādassāti| pāsādamāruyha paikkhati pāsādā paikkhati, āsanai upavisitvā paikkhati āsanā paikkhatīti avadhivacanicchāyaṃ pañ‍camī|

pucchānākhyānaisu kutau bhavaṃ? pātaṭaliputtasmāti| tathā daisakālamānaipi pāṭaliputtasmā rājagahaṃ satta yaujanāni, sattasu yaujanaisūti vā| aivaṃ itau tiṇṇaṃ māsāna-mac‍cayaināti, kicchāladdhanti guṇai pañ‍camī| kicchaina mai adhigatanti haitumhi karaṇai vā tatiyā| aivaṃ thaukā muttau, thaukaina muttauti| thaukaṃ calatīti kiriyāvisaisanai kammani dutiyā|

dūranti katthayaugaipi savisayaiva pañ‍camīchaṭṭhiyau siyuṃ, dūraṃ gāmasmā, antikaṃ gāmasmā, dūraṃ gāmassa, antikaṃ gāmassāti, dūranti katthaihi tu sabbāva savisayai siyuṃ bādhakābhāvā dūrau gāmau, antikau gāmautvaivamādi|

kaici  panāhu ‘asattavacanahaitaihi pāṭipadikatthai dutiyātatiyāpañ‍camīsattamiyau, sattavacanaihi tu sabbāva savisayai’ti, tai panañ‍ñaihaiva paṭikkhittā| dūraṃ maggau, antikaṃ maggauti kiriyāvisaisanaṃ… bhūdhātussa gammamānattā| visuddhau laubhanīyaihi dhammaihi, parimuttau sau dukkhasmā vivic‍caiva kāmaihi, gambhīratau ca puthulatau ca yaujanaṃ, āyāmaina yaujanaṃ, tatau pabhuti, yatau sarāmi attānanti savisayaiva vibhattiyau|

27. apaparīhi vaj‍janai|

vaj‍janai vattamānaihi apaparīhi yaugai pañ‍camī hauti| apa sālāya āyanti vāṇijā, pari sālāya āyanti vāṇijā, sālaṃ vaj‍jaitvāti atthau| vaj‍janaiti kiṃ? rukkhaṃ parivij‍jautatai vij‍ju| āpāṭaliputtasmā vassi daivauti mariyādā-bhividhīsvavadhi visayaiva pañ‍camī, vinā pāṭaliputtaina saha vaiti visaisau, aivaṃ yāva pāṭaliputtasmā vassi daivauti|

28. paṭinidhipaṭidānaisu patinā|

paṭinidhimhi paṭidānai ca vattamānaina patinā yaugai nāmasmā pañ‍camī vibhatti hauti| buddhasmā pati sāriputtau, ghatamassa taisasmā pati dadāti, paṭinidhipaṭidānaisūti kiṃ? rukkhaṃ pati vij‍jautatai|

29. ritai dutiyā ca|

ritaisaddaina  yaugai nāmasmā dutiyā hauti pañ‍camī ca| ritai saddhammaṃ, ritai saddhammā|

30. vinā-ñ‍ñatra tatiyā ca|

vinā-ñ‍ñatrasaddaihi yaugai nāmasmā tatiyā ca hauti dutiyā pañ‍camiyau ca| vinā vātaina, vinā vātaṃ, vinā vātasmā, añ‍ñatra aikaina piṇḍapātanīhārakaina, añ‍ñatra dhammaṃ, añ‍ñatra dhammā|

31. puthanānāhi|

aitaihi yaugai tatiyā hauti pañ‍camī ca| puthagaiva janaina, puthagaiva janasmā, janaina nānā, janasmā nānā|

32. sattamyādhārai|

kiriyādhāra bhūta kattu kammānaṃ dhāraṇaina yau kiriyāyādhārau tasmiṃ kārakai nāmasmā sattamī hauti| kaṭai nisīdati (daivadattau), thāliyaṃ audanaṃ pacati, ākāsai sakunā, bhilaisu tailaṃ, gaṅgāyaṃ vajau|

33. nimittai|

nimittatthai sattamī hauti| ajinamhi hañ‍ñatai dīpi, musāvādai pācitthiyaṃ|

34. yabbhāvaubhāvalakkhaṇaṃ|

yassa  bhāvau bhāvantarassa lakkhaṇaṃ bhavati, tatau sattamī hauti| gāvīsu duyhamānāsu gatau, duddhāsu āgatau| bhāvauti kiṃ? yau jaṭāhi sau bhuñ‍jati| bhāvalakkhaṇanti kiṃ? yau bhuñ‍jati sau daivadattau, ‘‘akālai vassati tassa, tālai tassa na vassatī’’ti visayasattamī|

35. chaṭṭhī cānādarai|

yassa bhāvau bhāvantarassa lakkhaṇaṃ bhavati, tatau chaṭṭhī bhavati sattamī ca anādarai gamyamānai| ‘‘ākauṭayantau sau naiti, sivirājassa paikkhatau’’, ‘‘mac‍cu gacchati ādāya, paikkhamānai mahājanai’’|

gun‍naṃ sāmīti sambandhai chaṭṭhī, gausu sāmīti visayasattamī, aivaṃ gun‍namissarau, gausvissarau, gun‍naṃ adhipati, gausu adhipati, gun‍naṃ dāyādau, gausu dāhādau, dun‍naṃ sakkhi, gausu sakkhi, gun‍naṃ patibhū, gausu patibhū, gun‍naṃ pasūtau, gausu pasūtau, kusalā nac‍cagītassa, kusalā nac‍cagītai, āyuttau kaṭakaraṇassa, āyuttau kaṭakaraṇaiti|

tathādhāravacanicchāyaṃ sattamī, bhikkhūsu abhivādainti, muddhani dhumbitvā, bāhāsu gahaitvā, hatthaisu piṇḍāya caranti, pathaisu gacchanti, kadalīsu gajai rakkhantīti| ñāṇasmiṃ pasan‍nauti visayasattamī, ñāṇaina pasan‍nauti karaṇai tatiyā, aivaṃ ñāṇasmiṃ ussuk‍kau ñāṇaina ussuk‍kauti|

36. yatau niddhāraṇaṃ|

jātiguṇakiriyāhi  samudāyataikadaisassa puthak‍karaṇaṃ niddhāraṇaṃ| yatau taṃ karīyati, tatau chaṭṭhīsattamiyau haunti| sālayau sūkadhañ‍ñānaṃ pathyatamā, sālayau sūkadhañ‍ñaisu pathyatamā, kaṇhā gāvīnaṃ sampan‍nakhīratamā, kaṇhā gāvīsu sampan‍nakhīratamā, gacchataṃ dhāvantau sīghatamau, gacchantaisu dhāvantau sīghatamau| sīlamaiva sutā saiyyauti avadhimhiyaiva pañ‍camī|

37. paṭhamātthamattai|

nāmassābhidhaiyyamattai paṭhamāvibhatti hauti| rukkhau| itthi pumā napuṃsakanti liṅgampi saddatthauva, tathā dauṇau khārī āḷhakanti parimāṇampi saddatthauva, aikau dvai bahavauti saṅkhyāpi saddatthauva|

38. āmantaṇai|

satau saddainābhimukhīkaraṇamāmantaṇaṃ| tasmiṃ visayai paṭhamā vibhatti hauti| bhaupurisa, bhauk‍katthi, bhau napuṃsaka|

39. chaṭṭhī sambandhai|

kiriyākārakasañ‍jātau assaidambhāvahaitukau sambandhau nāma| tasmiṃ chaṭṭhī vibhatti hauti| rañ‍ñau purisau, sarati raj‍jassāti sambandhai chaṭṭhī, raj‍jasambandhiniṃ satiṃ karautīti atthau, kammavacanicchārantu dutiyāva sarati raj‍jaṃ| tathā rajakassa vatthaṃ  dadāti, paharatau piṭṭhiṃ dadāti, bālau pūrati pāpassa, amac‍cai tāta jānāhi, dhīrai atthassa kauvidai, divasassa tikkhattuṃ, sakiṃ pakkhassa, pūraṃ hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇassa kumbhantvaivamādi|

kitakappayaugai kaktukammaisu bahulaṃ sambandhavacanicchāyaṃ chaṭṭhī, sādhu sammatau bahujanassa, suppaṭividdhā buddhānaṃ dhammadhātu, dhammassa guttau maidhāvī, amataṃ taisaṃ paribhuttaṃ, tassa bhavanti vattārau, avisaṃvādakau laukassa, alaj‍jīnaṃ nissāya, catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādautvaivamādi|

kattukammavacanicchāyantu tatiyā dutiyāyau ca, sañ‍cattau pitarā ahaṃ, sarasi tvaṃ aivarūpiṃ vācaṃ bhāsitā, bhagavantaṃ dassanāyatvaivamādi|

40. tulyatthaina vā tatiyā|

tulyatthaina yaugai chaṭṭhī hauti tatiyā vā, tulyau pitu, tulyau pitarā, sadisau pitu, sadisau pitarā, iha kathaṃ tatiyā na hauti? aj‍junassa tulā natthi, kaisavassupamā na caiti, naitai tulyatthā, kiñ‍jarahi tulyānamaupammatthā|

41. atau yaunaṃ ṭāṭai|

akāranthatau nāmasmā yaunaṃ ṭāṭai haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ, ṭakārā sabbadaisatthā, buddhā buddhai, atauti kiṃ? kañ‍ñāyau, itthiyau, vadhuyau, idha kasmā na bhavati aggayau| avidhānasāmatthiyā|

42. ninaṃ vā|

akārantatau  nāmasmā ninaṃ ṭāṭai vā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ| rūpā, rūpai, rūpāni, atautvaiva aṭṭhīni|

43. smāsmiṃnaṃ|

akārantatau nāmasmā smāsmin‍naṃ ṭāṭai vā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ| buddhā buddhasmā, buddhai buddhasmiṃ, atautvaiva aggismā aggismiṃ|

44. sassāya catutthiyā|

akārantatau parassa sassa catutthiyā āyau hauti vā| buddhāya buddhassa, bhiyyau tādatthyaiyaivāyamāyau dissatai, k‍vacidaivañ‍ñattha, atautvaiva isissa, catutthiyāti kiṃ? buddhassa mukhaṃ, attatthanti atthasaddaina samāsau|

sabbāditaupi smāsmiṃsānaṃ ṭāṭaiāyā hauntaiva… niruttikārānumatattā buddhavacanai sandassanavatau ca, tatraudamudāharaṇaṃ ‘asmā laukā paramhā ca, ubhayā dhaṃsatai narau’, ‘tyāhaṃ mantai paratthaddhau’, ‘yāyaiva khau panatthāya āgacchaiyyāthau tamaivatthaṃ sādhukaṃ manasikaraiyyāthau’ti|

45. ghapataikasmiṃ nādīnaṃ yayā|

ghapatau nādīnamaikasmiṃ yayā hauni yathāk‍kamaṃ| kañ‍ñāya, rattiyā, itthiyā, dhainuyā, vadhuyā, aikasminti kiṃ? kañ‍ñāhi, rattīhi|

46. ssā vā taitimāmūhi|

ghapasañ‍ñaihi  taitimāmūhi nādīnamaikasmiṃssā vā hauti, tassā kataṃ, tassā dīyatai, tassā nissaṭaṃ, tassā pariggahau, tassā patiṭṭhitaṃ, tāya vā, aivaṃ aitissā aitāya, imissā imāya, amussā amuyā, aitaihīti kiṃ? sabbāya, nādīnaṃ tvaiva? sā, ghapatautvaiva? tāhi amūhi|

47. naṃmhi nuka dvādīnaṃ sattarasan‍naṃ|

dvādīnaṃ sattarasan‍naṃ saṅkhyānaṃ nuka hauti naṃmhi vibhattamhi, dvin‍naṃ catun‍naṃ, pañ‍can‍naṃ, aivaṃ yāva aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ, ukārau uc‍cāraṇatthau, kakārau antāvayavatthau, taina naṃmhi na dīghau|

48. basukatin‍naṃ|

naṃmhi bahunau katissa ca nuka hauti, bahun‍naṃ, katin‍naṃ|

49. ṇṇaṃṇṇan‍naṃ titau jhā|

jhasañ‍ñā titau naṃvacanassa ṇṇaṃṇṇan‍naṃ hauti, tiṇṇaṃ, tiṇṇan‍naṃ, jhāti kiṃ tissan‍naṃ|

50. ubhin‍naṃ|

ubhā naṃvacanassa in‍naṃ hauti, ubhin‍naṃ|

51. suña sassa|

nāmasmā sassa suña hauti, buddhassa, dvisakārapāṭhaina siddhai lāghavatthamidaṃ|

52. ssaṃssāssāyaisvitarai kañ‍ñaibhimānami|

ssamādīsvitarādīnami  hauti, itarissaṃ, itarissā, aikissaṃ, aikissā, añ‍ñissaṃ, añ‍ñissā, aitissaṃ, aitissā, aitissāya, imissaṃ, imissā, imissāya, aisviti kiṃ? itarāya, aisanti kiṃ? sabbassaṃ, sabbassā|

53. tāya vā|

ssamādīsu tassā vā i hauti, tissaṃ tassaṃ, tissā tassā, tissāya tassāya, ssaṃssāssāyaisvitvaiva? tāya|

54. taitimātau sassa ssāya|

tāaitāimātau sassa ssāyau hauti vā| tassāya tāya, aitissāya aitāya, imissāya imāya|

55. ratyādīhi ṭau sminau|

ratyādīhi sminau ṭau hauti vā, rattau rattiyaṃ, ādau ādismiṃ|

56. suhisubhassau|

ubhassa suhisvau hauti| ubhausu, ubhauhi|

57. ltupitādinamā simhi|

ltuppac‍cayantānaṃ pitādīnaṃ ca ā hauti simhi| kattā, pitā| pitu, mātu, bhāvu, mītu, duhitu, jāmātu, nattu, hautu, pautu|

58. gai a ca|

ltupitādīnaṃ  a hauti gai ā ca, bhau katta, bhau kattā, bhau pita, bhau pitā|

59. ayunaṃ vā dīghau|

a i u ic‍caisaṃ vā dīghau hauti gai parai tiliṅgai| bhau purisā, bhau purisa, bhau aggī, bhau aggi, bhau bhikkhū, bhau bhikkhū|

60. ghabrahmāditai|

ghatau brahmāditau ca gassai vā hauti| bhauti kañ‍ñai, bhauti kañ‍ñā, bhau brahmai, bhau brahma, bhau khattai, bhau khatta, bhau isai, bhau isi, bhau sakhai, bhau sakha| sakhi sakhīti itthiyaṃ siddhamaiva| ākatigaṇau-yaṃ, aivamañ‍ñatrāpi|

61. nammādīhi|

ammādīhi gassai na hauti| bhauti ammā, bhauti an‍nā, bhauti ambā|

62. rassau vā|

ammādīnaṃ gai rassau hauti vā| bhauti amma, bhauti ammā|

63. ghau ssaṃ, ssā, ssāyaṃ tiṃsu|

ssamādīsu ghau rassau hauti| tassaṃ, tassā, tassāya, taṃ, sabhatiṃ, aisviti kiṃ? tāya, sabhāya|

64. aikavacana yausva-ghaunaṃ|

aikavacanai  yausu ca ghaaukārantavaj‍jitā naṃ nāmānaṃ rassau hauti tiliṅgai| itthiṃ, itthiyā, itthiyau, vadhuṃ vadhuyā, vadhuyau, daṇḍiṃ, daṇḍinā, daṇḍinau, sayambhuṃ, sayambhunā, sayambhuvau, aghau nanti kiṃ? kañ‍ñāya, kañ‍ñāyau, auggahaṇamuttaratthaṃ|

65. gai vā|

aghaunaṃ gai vā rassau hauti tiliṅgai, itthi, itthī, vadhu, vadhū, daṇḍi, daṇḍī, sayambhu, sayambhū| aghaunaṃtvaiva? bhauti kañ‍ñā, bhau gau|

66. sismiṃ nānapuṃsakassa|

napuṃsakavaj‍jitassa nāmassa sismiṃ rassau na hauti| itthī, daṇḍī, vadhū, sayambhū| sisminti kiṃ? itthiṃ, anapuṃsakassāti kiṃ? daṇḍi kulaṃ|

67. gaussāgasihinaṃsu gāvagavā|

gasihinaṃvaj‍jitāsu vibhattīsu gausaddassa gāvagavā haunti| (gāvaṃ, gavaṃ), gāvau, gavau, gāvaina, gavaina, gāvassa, gavassa, gāvasmā, gavasmā, gāvai, gavai| agasihinaṃsūti kiṃ? bhau gau, gau tiṭṭhati, gauhi, gaunaṃ|

68. sumhi vā|

gaussa sumhi gāvagavā haunti vā| gāvaisu, gavaisu, gausu|

69. gavaṃ saina|

gaussa  sai vā gavaṃ hauti saha saina| gavaṃ, gāvassa, gavassa|

70. gun‍naṃ ca naṃnā|

naṃvacanaina saha gaussa gun‍naṃ hauti gavaṃca vā| gun‍naṃ, gavaṃ, gaunaṃ|

71. nāssā|

gautau nāssa ā hauti vā| gāvā, gavā, gāvaina, gavaina|

72. gāvumhi|

aṃvacanai gaussa gāvha vā hauti| vāvhaṃ, gāvaṃ, gavaṃ| gaussa gauṇādaisau na katau… saddantarattā|

73. yaṃ pītau|

pasañ‍ñītau aṃvacanassa yaṃ vā hauti| itthiyaṃ, itthiṃ| pītauti kiṃ? daṇḍiṃ, rattiṃ|

74. naṃ jhītau|

jhasañ‍ñītau aṃvacanassa naṃ vā hauti| daṇḍinaṃ, daṇḍiṃ| kathaṃ ‘buddhaṃ ādic‍cabandhuna’nti? yaugavibhāvā| jhāti kiṃ? itthiṃ| īti kiṃ? aggiṃ|

75. yaunaṃ naunai pumai|

jhītau  yaunaṃ naunai vā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ pul‍liṅgai| daṇḍīnau, daṇḍinai, daṇḍī, jhītau tvaiva? itthiyau, pumaiti kiṃ? daṇḍīni kulāni|

76. nau|

jhītau yaunaṃ nau vā hauti pul‍liṅgai| daṇḍinau tiṭṭhanti, daṇḍinau passa, daṇḍī vā|

77. sminau ni|

jhītau smiṃvacanassa ni hauti vā, daṇḍini, daṇḍismiṃ, jhītau tvaiva? aggismiṃ|

78. ambvādīhi|

ambuādīhi sminauni hauti vā, phalaṃ patati ambuni, pupphaṃ yathā paṃsuni ātapai kataṃ, vātvaiva? ambumhi, paṃsumhi|

79. kammāditau|

kammāditau sminau ni hauti vā| kammani kammai| kamma, camma, vaisma, bhasma (asma), brahma, atta, ātuma, ghamma, muddha| kammāditauti kiṃ? buddhai|

80. nāssainau|

kammāditau nāvacanassa ainau vā hauti| kammaina, kammanā, cammaina, cammanā, kammāditautvaiva? buddhaina|

81. jhalā sassa nau|

jhalatau  sassa nau vā hauti| agginau aggissa, daṇḍinau daṇḍissa, bhikkhunau bhikkhussa, sayambhunau sayambhussa,

kathaṃ ‘yau ca sissau mahāmunai’ ti? (3) ‘‘itau k‍vaci sassa ṭānubandhau’’ti brahmādīsu pāṭhā sassa ai ṭānubandhau|

82. nā smāssa|

jhalatau smāssa nā hauti vā| agginā aggismā, daṇḍinā daṇḍismā, bhikkhunā bhikkhusmā, sayambhunā sayambhusmā|

83. lā yau naṃ vau pumai|

latau yaunaṃ vau hauti vā pul‍liṅgai| bhikkhavau bhikkhū, sayambhuvau sayambhūpumaiti kiṃ? āyūni|

84. jantvāditau nau ca|

jantvāditau yaunaṃ nau hauti vau ca vā pul‍liṅgai| jantunau, jantavau jantuyau, gautrabhunau, gautrabhuvau gautrabhū| sahabhunau, sahabhuvau sahabhū|

85. kūtau|

kūpac‍cayantatau yaunaṃ nau vā hauti pul‍liṅgai, vidunau vidū, viñ‍ñunau viñ‍ñū, sabbañ‍ñunau sabbañ‍ñū|

86. dhalāpau-musmā|

amusaddatau yaunaṃ laupauva hauti pul‍liṅgai, amū, pumaitvaiva? amuyau amūni| vaupavādauyaṃ|

87. na nau sassa|

amusmā  sassa nau na hauti, amussa, nauti kiṃ? amuyā|

88. yaulaupanisu dīghau|

yaunaṃ laupai nisu ca dīghau hauti, aṭṭhī aṭṭhīni, yaulaupanisūti kiṃ? rattiyau|

89. sunaṃhisu|

aisu nāmassa dīghau hauti| aggīsu, aggīnaṃ, aggīhi|

90. pañ‍cādīnaṃ cuddasan‍nama|

pañ‍cādīnaṃ cuddasan‍naṃ sunaṃhisva hauti| pañ‍casu, pañ‍can‍naṃ, pañ‍cahi, chasu, chan‍naṃ, chahi, aivaṃ yāva aṭṭharasā|

91. yvādau ntussa|

yvādīsu ntussa a hauti| guṇavantā, guṇavantaṃ, guṇavantai, guṇavantaina ic‍cādi, yvā-dauti kiṃ? guṇavā tiṭṭhati|

92. ntassa ca ṭa vaṃsai|

aṃsaisu ntappac‍cayassa ṭa hauti vā ntussa ca| yaṃ yaṃ hi rāja bhajati santaṃvāyadi vā asaṃ, kic‍cā-nakubbassa karaiyya kic‍caṃ, himavaṃva pabbataṃ, sujātimantaupi ajātimassa| yaugavibhāgaināñ‍ñatrāpi| cakkhumā andhitā haunti, vaggumudātīriyā pana bhikkhū vaṇṇavā haunti|

93. yausu jhissa pumai|

jhasañ‍ñassa  issa yausu vā ṭa hauti pul‍liṅgai| aggayau aggī, jhaggahaṇaṃ kiṃ? ikārantasamudāyassa ṭa mā siyābhi, rattiyau, iggahaṇaṃ kiṃ? daṇḍinau, pumaiti kiṃ? aṭṭhīni|

94. vaivausu lussa|

lasañ‍ñassa ussa vaivausu ṭa hauti| bhikkhavai, bhikkhavau, vaivausūti kiṃ? jantuyau, uggahaṇaṃ kiṃ? sayambhuvau|

95. yaumhi vā k‍vaci|

yaumhi k‍vaci lasañ‍ñassa ussa vā ṭa hauti| haitayau, nandanti taṃ kurayau dassanaina, aj‍jaiva taṃ kurayau pāpayatu| vāti kiṃ? haituyau|

96. pumālapanai vaivau|

lasañ‍ñatau utau yaussālapanai vaivau haunti vā pul‍liṅgai| bhikkhavai, bhikkhavau bhikkhū, pumaiti kiṃ āyūni, ālapanaiti kiṃ? jantuyau tiṭṭhanti, lutautvaiva? dhainuyau, sayambhuvau|

97. smāhismiṃ naṃ mhābhimhi|

nāmasmā paraisaṃ smāhismin‍naṃ mhā bhimhi vā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ| buddhamhā buddhasmā, buddhaibhi buddhaihi, buddhamhi buddhasmiṃ, bahulādhikārāapavādavisayaipi, dasasahassimhi dhātuyā|

98. suhisvassai|

akārantassa  suhisvai hauti| buddhaisu buddhaihi|

99. sabbādīnaṃ naṃmhīca|

akārantānaṃ sabbādīnaṃ ai hauti naṃmhi suhisu ca| sabbaisaṃ, sabbaisu, sabbaihi, sabbādīnanti kiṃ? buddhānaṃ, assaitvaiva? amūsaṃ| sabba katara katama ubhaya itara añ‍ña añ‍ñatara añ‍ñatama (4) ‘‘pubbaparā-varadakkhiṇuttarā-dharāni vavatthāyamasañ‍ñāyaṃ’’ (pā,1,1,34) ya tya ta aita ima amu kiṃ aika tumha amha|

100. saṃsānaṃ|

sabbāditau naṃvacanassa saṃsānaṃ haunti| sabbaisaṃ, sabbaisānaṃ|

101. ghapā sassa ssā vā|

sabbādīnaṃ ghapatau sassa ssā vā hauti, sabbassā sabbāya, paggahaṇamuttaratthaṃ|

102. sminau ssaṃ|

sabbādīnaṃ ghapatau sminau ssaṃ vā hauti, sabbassaṃ sabbāya, amussaṃ amuyā|

103. yaṃ|

ghapatau sminau yaṃ vā hauti, kañ‍ñāyaṃ kañ‍ñāya, rattiyaṃ rattiyā, vadhuyaṃ vadhuyā, sabbāyaṃ sabbāya, amuyaṃ amuyā|

104. tiṃ sabhāparisāya|

sabhāparisāhi  sminau tiṃ vā hauti, sabhatiṃ sabhāya, parisatiṃ parisāya|

105. padādīhi si|

aihi sminau si hauti vā, padasi padasmiṃ, bilasi bilasmiṃ|

106. nāssa sā|

padādīhi nāssa sā hauti vā, padasā padaina, bilasā bilaina|

107. kaudhādīhi|

aihi nāssa sā hauti vā, kaudhasā kaudhaina, atthasā atthaina|

108. ataina|

akārantatau parassa nāvacanassa ainādaisau hauti, buddhaina, atauti kiṃ? agginā|

109. sissau|

akārantatau nāmasmā sissa au hauti, buddhau, atautvaiva? aggi|

110. k‍vacai vā|

akārantatau nāmasmā sissa ai hauti vā k‍vaci, vanappagumbai yathā phussitaggai| apavādavisayaipi bahulaṃvīdhānā-sukhai dukkhai| vāti kiṃ? vanappagumbau k‍vacīti kiṃ? pakkhai sabbattha mā hautu|

111. aṃ napuṃsakai|

akārantatau nāmasmā sissa aṃ hauti napuṃsakaliṅgai| rūpaṃ|

112. yaunaṃ ni|

akārantatau nāmasmā yaunaṃ ni hauti napuṃsakai| sabbāni rūpāni, nic‍cavidhānai phalaṃ aikac‍cādisabbādīnaṃ paṭhamāyaṃ|

113. jhalā vā|

jhalatau yaunaṃ ni hauti vā napuṃsakai, aṭṭhīni aṭṭhī, āyūni āyū|

114. laupau|

jhalatau yaunaṃ laupau hauti, aṭṭhī, āyū, aggī, bhikkhū, jhalātvaiva? aggayau| pagaiva kasmā na hauti? antaraṅgattā akārassa|

115. jantuhaitvīghapaihi vā|

jantuhaitūhi īkārantaihi ghapa sañ‍ñaihi ca paraisaṃ yaunaṃ vā laupau hauti, jantū jantuyau, haitū haituyau, daṇḍī daṇḍiyau, kañ‍ñā kañ‍ñāyau, rattī rattiyau, itthī itthiyau, dhainū dhainuyau, vadhū vadhuyau|

116. yai passivaṇṇassa|

pasañ‍ñassa  ivaṇṇassa laupau hauti vā yakārai, ratyau ratyā, ratyaṃ, paukkharañ‍ñau, paukkharañ‍ñā, paukkharañ‍ñaṃ, vā tvaiva? rattiyau, passāti kiṃ? daṇḍiyau, ivaṇṇassāti kiṃ? dhainuyau vadhuyau| kathaṃ ‘anuñ‍ñātau ahaṃ matyā’ti? ‘yai passā’ti yaugavibhāgā|

117. gasīnaṃ|

nāmasmā gasīnaṃ laupau hauti vijjhantarābhāvai, bhau purisa, ayaṃ, daṇḍī|

118. asaṅkhyaihi sabbāsaṃ|

avij‍jamānasaṅkhyaihi parāsaṃ sabbāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ laupau hauti, ca vā aiva aivaṃ, aitasmāyaiva liṅgā asaṅkhyaihi syādyuppatti anumīyatai|

119. aikatthatāyaṃ|

aikatthībhāvai sabbāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ laupau hauti bahulaṃ, puttīyati, rājapurisau, vāsiṭṭhau, k‍vaci na hauti bahulaṃ vidhānā-parantapau, bhagandarau, parassapadaṃ, attanaupadaṃ, gavampati, daivānaṃpiyatissau, antaivāsī, janaisutau mamattaṃ māmakau|

226. smimhi tumhāmhānaṃ tayimayi| smimhi tumhaamusaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ tayimayi haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ, tayi, mayi|

227. aṃmhi taṃ maṃ tavaṃ mamaṃ|

aṃmhi  tumhaamhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ taṃ maṃ tavaṃ mamaṃ haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ, taṃ, maṃ, tavaṃ, mamaṃ,

228. nāsmāsu tayāmayā|

nāsmāsu tumhaamhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ tayāmayā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ, tayā kataṃ, mayā kataṃ, tayā nissaṭaṃ, mayā nissaṭaṃ|

229. tava mama tuyhaṃ mayhaṃ sai|

sai tumhaamhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ tava mama tuyhaṃ mayhaṃ haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ, tava, tuyhaṃ, mama, mayhaṃ|

230. ṅaṃṅākaṃ naṃmhi|

naṃmhi tumhaamhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ ṅaṃṅākaṃ haunti pac‍caikaṃ, tumhaṃ, tumhākaṃ, amhaṃ, amhākaṃ, yathāsaṅkhyamatra na vivacchatai|

231. dutiyai yaumhi vā|

tumhaamhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ pac‍caikaṃ ṅaṃṅākaṃ vā haunti yaumhi dutiyai, tumhaṃ, tumhākaṃ, tumhai, amhaṃ, amhākaṃ, amhai|

232. apādādau padataikavākyai|

idamadhikataṃ vaiditabbaṃ| paj‍jatai-nainatthauti padaṃ-syādyantaṃ tyādyantaṃ ca, padasamūhau vākyaṃ|

233. yaunaṃhisvapañ‍camyā vaunau|

apañ‍camiyā  yaunaṃhisvapādādau vattamānānaṃ padasmā paraisaṃ aikavākyai ṭhitānaṃ tumhāmhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ vaunau haunti vā yathāk‍kamaṃ, tiṭṭhatha vau, tiṭṭhatha tumhai, tiṭṭhāma nau, tiṭṭhāma mayaṃ, passati vau, passati tumhai, passati nau, passati amhai, dīyatai vau, dīyatai tumhaṃ, dīyatai nau, dīyatai amhaṃ, dhanaṃ vau, dhanaṃ tumhaṃ, dhanaṃ nau dhanaṃ amhaṃ, kataṃ vau, kataṃ tumhaihi, kataṃ nau, kataṃ amhaihi, apañ‍camyāti kiṃ? nissaṭaṃ tumhaihi, nissaṭaṃ amhaihi, apādādautvaiva? ‘balañ‍ca bhikkhūnamanuppadin‍naṃ, tumhaihi puñ‍ñaṃ pasutaṃ anappakaṃ’, padatautvaiva? tumhai tiṭṭhatha, aikavākyaitvaiva? daivadattau tiṭṭhati gāmai, tumhai tiṭṭhatha nagarai, savibhattīnaṃtvaiva? arahati dhammau tumhādisānaṃ, arahati dhammau amhādisānaṃ|

234. taimai nāsai|

nāmhi sai ca apādādau vattamānānaṃ padasmā paraisaṃ aikavākyai ṭhitānaṃ tumhāmhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ taimai vā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ, kataṃ tai, kataṃ tayā, kataṃ mai, kataṃ mayā, dīyatai tai, dīyatai tava dīyatai mai, dīyatai mama, dhanaṃ tai, dhanaṃ tava, dhanaṃ mai, dhanaṃ mama|

235. anvādaisai|

kathitānukathanavisayai tumhaamha-saddānamādaisā nic‍caṃ bhavanti punabbidhānā, gāmau tumhaṃ pariggahau, athau janapadau vau pariggahau|

236. sapubbā paṭhamantā vā|

vij‍jamānapubbasmā  paṭhamantā paraisaṃ tumhaamhasaddānamādaisā vā haunti anvādaisaipi, gāmai paṭau tumhākaṃ, athau nagarai kambalau vau, athau nagarai kambalau tumhākaṃ, sapubbāti kiṃ? paṭau tumhākaṃ, athau kambalau vau, paṭhamantāti ki? paṭau nāgarai tumhākaṃ, athau kambalau gāmai vau|

237. na ca vā hā haivayaugai|

cādīhi yaugai tumhaamhasaddānamādaisā na haunti, gāmau tava ca pariggahau, mama ca pariggahau, gāmau tava vā pariggahau, mama vā pariggahau, gāmau tava ha pariggahau, mama ha pariggahau, gāmau tavāha pariggahau, mamāha pariggahau, gāmau tavaiva pariggahau, mamaiva pariggahau, aivaṃ sabbattha udāharitabbaṃ, yaugaiti kiṃ? gāmau ca tai pariggahau, nagarañ‍ca mai pariggahau|

238. dassanatthai nālaucanai|

dassanatthaisu ālaucanavaj‍jitaisu payuj‍jamānaisu tumhaamhasaddānamādaisā na haunti, gāmau tumhai uddissāgatau, gāmau amhai uddissāgatau, anālaucanaiti kiṃ? gāmau vau ālaucaiti, gāmau nau ālaucaiti|

293. āmantaṇaṃ pubbamasantaṃva| āmantaṇaṃ pubbamavij‍jamānaṃ viya hauti tumhāmhasaddānamādaisavisayai, daivadatta tava pariggahau, āmantaṇanti kiṃ? kambalau tai pariggahau, pubbamiti kiṃ? ‘mayaitaṃ sabbamakkhātaṃ, tumhākaṃ dvijapuṅgavā, parassa hi avij‍jamānattai ‘apādādau’ti paṭisaidhau na siyā| ivāti kiṃ? savanaṃ yathā siyā|

240. na sāmañ‍ñavacanamaikatthai|

samānādhikaraṇai paratau sāmañ‍ñavacanamāmantaṇamasantaṃ viya na hauti, māṇavaka jaṭilaka tai pariggahau| parassāvij‍jamānattaipi pubbarūpamupādāyādaisau hauti, sāmañ‍ñavacananti kiṃ? daivadatta māṇavaka tava pariggahau, aikatthaiti kiṃ? daivadatta yañ‍ñadatta tumhaṃ pariggahau|

241. bahūsu vā|

bahūsu vattamānamāmantaṇaṃ sāmañ‍ñavacanamaikatthai avij‍jamānaṃ viya vā na hauti, brāhmaṇā guṇavantau tumhākaṃ pariggahau, brāhmaṇā guṇavantau vau pariggahau|

iti mauggal‍lānai byākaraṇai vuttiyaṃ

syādikaṇḍau dutiyau|

(3) tatiyau kaṇḍau (samāsau)

1. syādi syādinaikatthaṃ|

syādyantaṃ syādyantaina sahaikatthaṃ hautīti idamadhikataṃ vaiditabbaṃ, sau ca bhin‍natthānamaikatthībhāvau samāsauti vuc‍catai|

2. asaṅkhyaṃvibhatti sampatti samīpa sākalyābhāva yathā pacchāyugapadatthai|

asaṅkhyaṃ, syādyantaṃ vibhatyādīnamatthai vattamānaṃ syādyantaina sahaikatthaṃ bhavati, tattha vibhatyatthai tāva itthīsu kathā pavattā adhitthi| sampatti dvidhā attasampatti samiddhi ca, sampan‍naṃ brahmaṃ sabrahmaṃ licchavīnaṃ, samiddhi bhikkhānaṃ subhikkhaṃ| samīpai kumbhassa samīpamupakumbhaṃ| sākalyaisatiṇamajjhauharati, sāgyadhītai| abhāvau sambandhibhaidā bahuvidhau, tatra iddhābhāvai-vigatā iddhi saddikānaṃ dussaddikaṃ, atthābhāvai-abhāvau makkhikānaṃ nimmakkhikaṃ, ahik‍kamābhāvai-atigatāni tiṇāni nittiṇaṃ, sampatyābhāvai-atigataṃ lahupāvuraṇaṃ atilahupāvuraṇaṃ, lahupāvuraṇassa nāyamupabhaugakālauti atthau| yathā aitthā-naikavidhau, tatra yauggatāyaṃ-anurūpaṃ surūpauvahati, vicchāyaṃ-anvaddhamāsaṃ, atthānativattiyaṃ-yathāsatti, sadisattai, sadisau kikhiyā sakikhi, ānupubbiyai-anujaiṭṭhaṃ, pacchādatthaianurathaṃ, yugapadatthai-sacak‍kaṃ nidhaihi|

3. yathā na tulyai|

yathāsaddau tulyatthai vattamānau syādyantaina sahaikatthau na bhavati, yathā daivadattau tathā yañ‍ñadattau|

4. yāvāvadhāraṇai|

yāvasaddau-vadhāraṇai vattamānau syādyantaina sahaikatthau bhavati, avadhāraṇa maittakatā paricchaidau, yāvāmattaṃ brāhmaṇai āmantaya, yāvajīvaṃ, avadhāraṇaiti kiṃ? yāva din‍naṃ tāva bhuttaṃ, nāvadhārayāmi kittakaṃ mayā bhuttanti|

5. payyapā  bahi tirau purai pacchā vā pañ‍camyā|

pariādayau pañ‍camyantaina sahaikatthā haunti vā, paripabbataṃ vassi daivau paripabbatā, apapabbataṃ vassi daivau apapabbatā, āpāṭaliputtaṃ vassi daivau āpāṭaliputtā, bahigāmaṃ bahi gāmā, tiraupabbataṃ tiraupabbatā, puraibhattaṃ puraibhattā, pacchābhattaṃ pacchābhattā, vaitādhikārau|

6. samīpāyāmaisvanu|

anusaddau sāmīpyai āyāmai ca vattamānau syādyantaina sahaikatthau hauti vā, anuvanamasani gatā, anugaṅgaṃ bārāṇasī, samīpāyāmaisvīti kiṃ? rakkhamanuvij‍jautatai vij‍ju|

7. tiṭṭhagvādīni|

tiṭṭhaguppabhutīni aikatthībhāvavisayai nipātīyantai, tiṭṭhantī gāvau yasmiṃ kālai tiṭṭhagu kālau, vahaggu kālau| āyatīgavaṃ, khalaiyavaṃ, lūnayavaṃ lūyamānayavamic‍cādi, cyantau paittha kaisā kaisi, daṇḍā daṇḍi, tathā vailāppabhāvanatthaupi, pātau nahānaṃ pātarahānaṃ, sāyaṃ nayānaṃ sāyanahānaṃ, pātakālaṃ sāyakālaṃ, pātamaighaṃ sāyamaighaṃ, pātamaggaṃ sāyamaggaṃ|

8. aurai pari paṭi pārai majjhai haiṭṭhuddhādhau-ntauvāchaṭṭhiyā| aurādayau saddā chaṭṭhiyantaina sahaikatthā vā haunti, aikārantattaṃ nipātanatau, auraigaṅgaṃ, parisikharaṃ, paṭisautaṃ, pāraiyamunaṃ, majjhaigaṅgaṃ, haiṭṭhāpāsādaṃ, uddhagaṅgaṃ, adhaugaṅgaṃ, anthaupāsādaṃ, puna vāvidhānā ‘gaṅgāaura’ mic‍cādīpi haunti|

9. taṃ napuṃsakaṃ|

yadaitamatik‍kantamaikatthaṃ, taṃ napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ vaiditabbaṃ, tathā caivaudāhaṭaṃ, vā k‍vaci bahulādhikārā, yathāparisaṃ yathāparisāya, sakāya sakāya parisāyāti atthau|

10. amādi|

amādi syādyantaṃ syādyantaina saha bahulamaikatthaṃ hauti gāmaṃ gatau gāmagatau, muhuttaṃ sukhaṃ muhuttasukhaṃ, vuttiyaivaupapadasamāsai kumbhakārau, sapākau, tantavāyau, varāharau| ntamānaktavantūti vākyamaiva, dhammaṃ suṇantau, dhammaṃ suṇamānau, audanaṃ bhuttavā|

rañ‍ñā hatau rājahatau, asinā chin‍nau asicchin‍nau, pitusadisau, pitusamau, sukhasahagataṃ, dadhinā upasittaṃ bhaujanaṃ dadhibhaujanaṃ, guḷaina missau audanau guḷaudanau, vuttipadainaivaupasittādikiriyāyākhyāpanatau natthāyuttatthatā| k‍vaci vuttiyaiva uragau, pādapau| k‍vaci vākyamaiva pharasunā chin‍navā, dassanaina pahātabbā|

buddhassa daiyyaṃ buddhadaiyyaṃ, yūpāya dāru yūpādāru, rajanāya dauṇi rajanadauṇi| idha na hauti saṅghassa dātabbaṃ| kathaṃ ‘aitadatthau aitadatthā aitadattha’nti? añ‍ñapadatthai bhavissati|

savaraihi bhayaṃ savarabhayaṃ, gāmaniggatau, maithunāpaitau, k‍vaci vuttiyaiva kammajaṃ, cittajaṃ, idha na hauti rukkhā patitau|

rañ‍ñau  purisau rājapurisau| bahulādhikārā ntamānaniddhāriyapūraṇabhāvatittatthaihi na hauti-mamānukubbaṃ, mamānukurumānau, gun‍naṃ kaṇhā sampan‍nakhīratamā, sissānaṃ pañ‍camau, paṭassa suk‍katā, k‍vaci hautaiva-vattamānasāmīpyaṃ, kathaṃ ‘brāhmaṇassa suk‍kā dantā’ti? sāpaikkhatāya na hauti| idha pana hautaiva ‘candanagandhau, nadighausau, kañ‍ñārūpaṃ, kāyasamphassau, phalarasau’ti, phalānaṃ tittau, phalānamāsitau, phasānaṃ suhitau|

brāhmaṇassa uc‍caṃ gaihanti sāpaikkhatāya na hauti, ‘rañ‍ñau pāṭaliputtakassa dhana’nti dhanasambandhai chaṭṭhīti pāṭaliputtakaina sambandhābhāvā na haissati, ‘rañ‍ñau gau ca assau ca purisau cā’ti bhin‍natthatāya vākyamaiva, ‘rañ‍ñau gavāssapurisā rājagavāssapurisā’ti vutti hautaivaikattibhāvai|

dānai sauṇḍau dānasauṇḍau, dhammaratau, dānābhiratau| k‍vaci vuttiyaiva kucchisayau, thalaṭṭhau, paṅkajaṃ, sarauruhaṃ| idha na hauti bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā, indriyaisu guttadvāratā, āsanai nisin‍nau, āsanai nisīditabbaṃ|

11. visaisanamaikatthaina|

visaisanaṃ syāntaṃ visaissaina syādyantaina samānādhikaraṇaina sahaikatthaṃ hauti, nīlañ‍ca taṃ uppalañ‍caiti nīluppalaṃ, chin‍nañ‍ca taṃ paruḷhañ‍caiti chin‍naparaḷhaṃ, satthīva satthī, satthī ca sā sāmā caiti satthisāmā, sīhauva sīhau, muni ca sau sīhau caiti munisīhau, sīlamaiva dhanaṃ sīladhanaṃ|

k‍vaci  vākyamaiva puṇṇau mantāṇiputtau, cittau gahapati| k‍vaci vuttiyaiva kaṇhasappau, lauhitasāli, visaisananti kiṃ? tacchakau sappau, aikatthainaiti kiṃ? kāḷamhā añ‍ñau| kathaṃ ‘pattajīvikau, āpan‍najīvikau, māsajātau’ti? añ‍ñapadatthai bhavissati|

12. naña|

nañic‍caitaṃ syādyantaṃ syādyantaina sahaikatthaṃ hauti, na brāhmaṇau abrāhmaṇau, bahulādhikāratau asamatthatthaihi, kaihici hauti ‘apunagaiyyā gāthā, anaukāsaṃ kāraitvā, amūlā mūlaṃ gantvā| īsaṃkaḷārau, īsaṃpiṅgalauti ‘syādi syādinai’ti samāsau, vākyamaiva vātippasaṅgābhāvā|

13. kupādayau nic‍camasyādividhimhi|

kusaddau pādayau ca syādyantaina sahaikatthā haunti nic‍caṃ syādividhivisayatau-ñattha, kucchitau brāhmaṇau kubrāhmaṇau, īsakaṃ uṇhaṃ kaduṇhaṃ, panāyakau, abhisaikau, pakaritvā, pakataṃ, duppurisau, duk‍kaṭaṃ, supurisau, sukataṃ, abhitthutaṃ, atitthutaṃ, ākaḷārau, ābaddhau|

(9) ‘‘pādayau gatādyatthai paṭhamāya’’| pagatau ācariyau pācariyau, pantaivāsī|

(10) ‘‘ac‍cādayau kantādyatthai dutiyāya’’| atik‍kantau mañ‍camatimañ‍cau, atimālau|

(11) ‘‘avādayau  kuṭṭhādyatthai tatiyāya’’| avakuṭṭhaṃ kaukilāya vanaṃ avakaukilaṃ, avamayūraṃ|

(12) ‘‘pariyādayau gilānādyatthai catutthiyā’’| parigilānau ajjhaināya pariyajjhainau|

(13) ‘‘nyādayau kantādyatthai pañ‍camiyā’’|

nikkhantau kausambiyā nik‍kausampi, asyādividhimhīti kiṃ? rukkhaṃ pati vij‍jautatai|

14. cī kriyatthaihi|

cīppac‍cayantau kiriyatthaihi syādyantaihi sahaikatthau hauti, malinīkariya|

15. bhūsanādarānādaraisvalaṃsāsā|

bhūsanādisvatthai svalamādayau saddā kiriyatthaihi syādyantaihi sahaikatthā haunti, alaṃkariya, sak‍kac‍ca, asak‍kac‍ca| bhūsanādīsūti kiṃ? alaṃbhutvā gatau, sak‍katvā gatau, asak‍katvā gatau, pariyattaṃ saubhanamasaubhananti atthau|

16. añ‍ñai ca|

añ‍ñai ca saddā kiriyatthaihi syādyantaihi saha bahulamaikatthā bhavanti, puraubhūya, tiraubhūya, tiraukariya, urasikariya, manasikariya, majjhaikariya, tuṇhībhūya|

17. vānaikañ‍ñatthai|

anaikaṃ  syādyantamañ‍ñassa padassatthai aikatthaṃ vā hauti, bahūni dhanāni yassa sau bahudhanau, lambā kaṇṇā yassa sau lambakaṇṇau, vajiraṃ pāṇimhi yassa sauyaṃ vajirapāṇi, mattā bahavau mātaṅgā aittha mattabahumātaṅgaṃ vanaṃ, āruḷhau vānarau yaṃ rukkhaṃ sau āruḷhavānarau, jitāni indriyāni yaina sau jitindriyau, din‍naṃ bhaujanaṃ yassa sau din‍nabhaujanau, apagataṃ kāḷakaṃ yasmā paṭā sau-yamapagatakāḷakau, upagatā dasa yaisaṃ tai upadasā, āsan‍nadasā, adūradasā, adhikadasā, tayau dasa parimāṇamaisaṃ tidasā, kathaṃ dasasaddau saṅkhyānai vattatai? parimāṇasaddasan‍nidhānā, yathā pañ‍ca parimāṇamaisaṃ pañ‍cakā sakunāti, dvai vā tayau vā parimāṇamaisaṃ dvattayau vāsaddatthai vā dvai vā tayau vā dvattayau|

dakkhiṇassā ca pubbassā ca disāya yadantarāḷaṃ dakkhiṇapubbā disā, dakkhiṇā ca sā pubbā cāti vā, saha puttaināgatau saputtau, salaumakau vij‍jamānalaumakauti atthau, aivaṃ sapakkhakau, atthī khīrā brāhmaṇīti atthisaddau vij‍jamānatthai nipātau, k‍vaci gatatthatāya padantarānamappayaugau, kaṇṭhaṭṭhā kāḷā assa kaṇṭhaikāḷau, auṭṭhassa mukhamiva mukhamassa auṭṭhamukhau, kaisasaṅghātau cūḷā assa kaisacūḷau, suvaṇṇavikārau alaṅkārau assa suvaṇṇālaṅkārau, papatitaṃ paṇṇamassa papatitapaṇṇau, papaṇṇau, avij‍jamānā puttā assa avij‍jamānaputtau, na santi puttā assa aputtai, k‍vaci na hauti pañ‍ca bhuttavantau assa bhātunau puttau assa atthīti bahulādhikāratau|

18. tattha gahaitvā taina paharitvā yuddhai sarūpaṃ|

sattamyantaṃ  tatiyantañ‍ca sarūpamanaikaṃ tattha gahaitvā taina paharitvā yuddhai-ñ‍ñapadatthai aikatthaṃ vā hauti, kaisaisu ca kaisaisu ca gahaitvā yuddhaṃ pavattaṃ kaisākaisi, daṇḍaihi ca daṇḍaihi ca paharitvā yuddhaṃ pavattaṃ daṇḍādaṇḍi, muṭṭhāmuṭṭhi, ‘‘ci vītiyārai’’ (3-51) ti ci samāsantau, ‘‘cismiṃ’’ (3.66) ti akārau| tattha tainaiti kiṃ? kāyañ‍ca kāyañ‍ca gahaitvā yuddhaṃ pavattaṃ| gahaitvā paharitvāti kiṃ? rathai ca rathai ca ṭhatvā yuddhaṃ pavatti| yuddhaiti kiṃ? hatthai ca hatthai ca gahaitvā sakhyaṃ pavattaṃ| sarūpanti kiṃ? daṇḍaihi ca musalaihi ca paharitvā yuddha pavattaṃ|

19. catthai|

anaikaṃ syādyantaṃ catthai aikatthaṃ vā bhavati| samuc‍cayaunvācayau itarītarayaugau samāhārau ca ca saddatthā, tattha samuc‍cayānvācayaisu naikatthībhāvau sambhavati, taisu hi samuc‍cayau añ‍ñamañ‍ñanirapaikkhā namattappadhānānaṃ katthaci kiriyāvisaisai cīyamānatā, yathā ‘dhavai ca khadirai ca palāsai ca chindā’ti| anvācayau ca yatthaikau padhānabhāvaina vidhīyatai aparau ca guṇabhāvaina, yathā ‘bhikkhañ‍cara gāvau cānayai’ti| itaradvayai tu sambhavati, taisu hi añ‍ñamañ‍ñasāpaikkhānamavayavabhadānugatau itarītarayaugau, yathā ‘sāriputtamauggal‍lānā’ti, assāvayavappadhānattā bahuvacanamaiva| añ‍ñamañ‍ñasāpaikkhānamaiva tirauhitāvayavabhaidau samudāyappadhānau samāhārau, yathā ‘chattupāhana’nti, assa pana samudāyappadhānattā aikavacanamaiva|

tai  ca samāhārītarītarayaugā bahulaṃ vidhānā niyatavisayāyaiva haunti, tatrāyaṃ visayavibhāgau nirutthipiṭakāgatau-pāṇitūriyayauggasainaṅgānaṃ, nic‍cavairīnaṃ, saṅkhyāparimāṇa-sañ‍ñānaṃ, khuddajantukānaṃ, pacanacaṇḍālānaṃ, caraṇasādhāraṇānaṃ, aikajjhāyanapāvacanānaṃ, liṅgavisaisānaṃ, vividhaviruddhānaṃ disānaṃ, nadīnañ‍ca nic‍caṃ samāhāraikattaṃ bhavati, tiṇarukkhapasusakunadhanadhañ‍ñabyañ‍janajanappadānaṃ vā, añ‍ñaisamitarītarayaugauva|

pāṇyaṅgānaṃ-cakkhusautaṃ, mukhanāsikaṃ, hanugīvaṃ, chavimaṃsalauhitaṃ, nāmarūpaṃ, jarāmaraṇaṃ| turiyaṅgānaṃ-alasatālambaraṃ, murajagaumukhaṃ, saṅkhadaiṇḍimaṃ, maddavikapāṇavikaṃ, gītavāditaṃ, sammatālaṃ| yauggaṅgānaṃ phālapācanaṃ, yuganaṅgalaṃ| sainaṅgānaṃ-asisattitaumarapiṇḍaṃ, asicammaṃ, dhanukalāpaṃ, paharaṇāvaraṇaṃ| nic‍cavairīnaṃ-ahinakulaṃ, bīḷālamūsikaṃ, kākaulūkaṃ, nāgasupaṇṇaṃ| saṅkhyāparimāṇa sañ‍ñānaṃ-aikakadukaṃ, dukatikaṃ, tikacatuk‍kaṃ, catuk‍kapañ‍cakaṃ, dasaikādasakaṃ| khuddajantukānaṃ kīṭapaṭaṅgaṃ, kunthakipil‍likaṃ, ḍaṃsamakasaṃ, makkhikakipil‍likaṃ| pacanacaṇḍālānaṃ-aurabbhikasūkarikaṃ, sākunti kamāgavikaṃ, sapākacaṇḍālaṃ, vainarathakāraṃ, puk‍kusa chavaḍāhakaṃ| caraṇasādhāraṇānaṃ-atisabhāradvājaṃ, kaṭhakalāpaṃ, sīlapañ‍ñāṇaṃ, samathavipassanaṃ, vij‍jācaraṇaṃ| aikajjhāyanapāvacanānaṃ dīghamajjhimaṃ, aikuttarasaṃyuttakaṃ, khandhakavibhaṅgaṃ| liṅgavisaisānaṃ-itthipukhaṃ, dāsidāsaṃ, cīvarapiṇḍapātasaināsanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhāraṃ, tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ, ‘lābhī hauti cīvarapiṇḍapātasainā- sanagilānappac‍cayabhaisaj‍japarikkhārāna’ntipi dissati| vividhaviruddhānaṃ kusalākusalaṃ, sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jaṃ, hīnappaṇītaṃ, kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ, chaikapāpakaṃ| disānaṃ pubbāparaṃ, dakkhiṇuttaraṃ, pubbadakkhiṇaṃ, pubbuttaraṃ, adharuttaraṃ, aparadakkhiṇaṃ, aparuttaraṃ| nadīnaṃ-gaṅgāyamunaṃ, mahisarabhu|

tiṇavisaisānaṃ-kāsakusaṃ kāsakusā, usīrabīraṇaṃ usīrabīraṇā, muñ‍japabbajaṃ muñ‍japabbajaṃ muñ‍japabbajā| rukkhavisaisānaṃ khadirapalāsaṃ khadirapalāsā, vaussakaṇṇaṃ dhavāssakaṇṇā, pilakkhanigraudhaṃ pilakkhanigraudhā, assatthakapitthanaṃ assatthakapitthanā, sākasālaṃ sākasālā| pasuvisaisānaṃ-gajagavajaṃ gajagavajā, gaumahisaṃ gaumahisā, aiṇaiyyagaumahisaṃ aiṇaiyyagaumahisā, aiṇaiyyavarāhaṃ aiṇaiyyavarāhā, ajaiḷakaṃ ajaiḷakā, kuk‍kurasūkaraṃ kuk‍kurasūkarā, hatthigavāssavaḷavaṃ hatthigavāssavaḷavā| sakunavisaisānaṃ-haṃsabalāvaṃ haṃsabalāvā, kāraṇḍavacak‍kavākaṃ kāraṇḍavacak‍kavākā, bakabalākaṃ bakabalākā| dhanānaṃ-hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaṃ hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇā, maṇisaṅkhamuttāvaiḷuriyaṃ maṇisaṅkhamuttāvaiḷuriyā, jātarūparajataṃ jātarūparajatā| dhañ‍ñānaṃ-sāliyavakaṃ sāliyavakā, tilamuggamāsaṃ tilamuggamāsā, nipphāvakulatthaṃ nipphāvakulatthā| byañ‍janānaṃ-sākasuvaṃ sākasuvā, gabyamāhisaṃ gabyamāhisā, aiṇaiyyavārāhaṃ aiṇaiyyavārāhā, migamāyūraṃ migamāyūrā| janapadānaṃ-kāsikausalaṃ kāsikausalā, vaj‍jimal‍laṃ vaj‍jimal‍lā, caitivaṃsaṃ caitivaṃsā, macchasūrasainaṃ macchasūrasainā, kurupañ‍cālaṃ kurupañ‍cālā| itarītarayaugau yathā-candimasūriyā, samaṇabrāhmaṇā mātāpitarau ic‍cādi|

aitasmiṃ  aikatthībhāvakaṇḍai yaṃ vuttaṃ pubbaṃ, tadaiva pubbaṃ nipatati kamātik‍kamai payaujanābhāvā| k‍vaci vipal‍lāsaupi hauti bahulādhikāratau, dantānaṃ rājā rājadantau, katthaci kamaṃ pac‍cānādarā pubbakālassāpi paranipātau, littavāsitau, naggamusitau, sittasammaṭṭhau, bhaṭṭhaluñ‍citau| catthai yadaikatthaṃ tattha kaici pubbapadaṃ bahudhā niyamainti, tadiha byabhicāradassāna na vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

20. samāhārai napuṃsakaṃ|

catthai samāhārai yadaikatthaṃ, taṃ napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ bhavati, tathācaivaudāhaṭaṃ, katthaci na hauti ‘sabhāparisāyā’ti ñāpakā, ādhipac‍caparivārau, chandapārisuddhi, paṭisandhipavattiyaṃ|

21. saṅkhyādi|

aikatthai samāhārai saṅkhyādi napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ bhavati, pañ‍cagavaṃ, catuppathaṃ, samāhārassaikattā aikavacanamaiva hauti, samāhāraitvaiva pañ‍cakāpālau pūvau, tiputtau|

22. k‍vacaikattañ‍ca chaṭṭhiyā|

chaṭṭhiyaikatthai k‍vaci napuṃsakattaṃ hautaikatthañ‍ca, salabhānaṃ chāyā salabhacchāyaṃ, aivaṃ sakuntānaṃ chāyā sakuntacchāyaṃ, pāsādacchāyaṃ pāsādacchāyā, gharacchāyaṃ gharacchāyā, amanussasabhāya napuṃsakaikattaṃ bhavati brahmasabhaṃ, daivasabhaṃ, indasabhaṃ, yakkhasabhaṃ, sarabhasabhaṃ, manussasabhāyaṃ pana khattiyasabhā, rājasabhā ic‍caivamādi, k‍vacīti kiṃ rājapurisau|

23. syādīsu rassau|

napuṃsakai  vattamānassa rassau hauti syādīsu| salabhacchāyaṃ, syādīsūti kiṃ? salabhacchāyai|

24. ghapassantassāppadhānassa|

antabhūtassa appadhānassa ghapassa syādīsu rassau hauti| bahumālau pausau, nik‍kausambi, ativāmauru, antassāti kiṃ? rājā kañ‍ñāpiyau, appadhānassāti kiṃ? rājakumārī brahmabandhū|

25. gaussu|

antabhūtassa appadhānassa gaussa syādīsu u hauti| cittagu, appadhānassātvaiva? sugau, antassātvaiva? gaukulaṃ|

26. itthiyamatvā|

itthiyaṃ vattamānatau akārantatau nāmasmā āpac‍cayau hauti| dhammadin‍nā|

37. nadāditau vī|

nadādīhi itthiyaṃ vīppac‍cayau hauti| nadī, mahī, kumārī, taruṇī, vāruṇī, gautamī| (14) ‘‘gautauvā’’ gāvī gau, ākatigaṇau-yaṃ, kaurau ‘‘ntantūnaṃ vīmhi tau vā’’ (3-36) ti visaisanatthau|

28. yakkhāditinī ca|

yakkhāditau  itthiyaṃ inī hauti vīca| yakkhinī yakkhī, nāginī nāgī, sīhinī sīhī|

26. ārāmikādīhi|

ārāmikāditau inī hautitthiyaṃ| ārāmikinī, anantarāyikinī, rājinī (15) ‘‘sañ‍ñāyaṃ mānusau’’ mānusinī, añ‍ñatra mānusī|

30. yuvaṇṇaihi nī|

itthiyamivaṇṇuvaṇṇantaihi nī hauti bahulaṃ| sadāpayatapāṇinī, daṇḍinī, bhikkhunī, khattabandhunī, paracittavidunī, mātuāditau kasmā na hauti? itthippac‍cayaṃ vināpi itthattābhidhānatau|

31. ktimhāñ‍ñatthai|

ktimhāñ‍ñatthaiyaiva itthiyaṃ nī hauti bahulaṃ| sāhaṃ ahiṃ sāratinī, tassā muṭṭhassatiniyā, sā gāvī vacchagiddhinī, añ‍ñatthaiti kiṃ? dhammarati|

32. gharaṇyādayau|

gharaṇippabhutayau nīppac‍cantāsādhavau bhavanti| gharaṇī, paukkharaṇī, īssa-ttaṃ nipātanā, (16) ‘‘ācariyā vā ya-laupau ca’’ ācarinī, ācariyā|

33. mātulāditvānī bhariyāyaṃ|

mātulāditau  bhariyāyamānī hauti| mātulānī, vāruṇānī, gahapatānī, ācariyānī, (17) ‘‘abhariyāyaṃ khattiyā vā’’ khattiyānī khattiyā, nadādipāṭhā bhariyāyantu khattiyī|

34. upamāsaṃhita sahita sañ‍ñata saha saphavāma lakkhaṇāditūrutū|

ūrusaddā upamānādipubbā ittiyamū hauti| karabhaurū, saṃhitaurū, sahitaurū, sañ‍ñataurū, sahaurū, saphaurū, vāmaurū, lakkhaṇaurū, ūtiyaugavibhāgā ū brahmabandhū|

35. yuvā ti|

yuvasaddatau ti hautitthiyaṃ| yuvati|

36. ntantūnaṃ vīmhitau vā|

vīmhi ntantūnaṃ tau vā hauti| gacchatī gacchantī, sīlavatī sīlavantī|

37. bhavatau bhautau|

vīmhi bhavatau bhautādaisau hauti vā| bhautī bhavantī|

38. gaussāvaṅa|

gausaddassa vīmhāvaṅa hauti| gāvī|

39. puthussa pathavaputhavā|

vīmhi  puthussa pathavaputhavā haunti| pathavī, puthavī, ṭhai pathavī|

40. samāsantva|

samāsantva iti cādhikarīyati|

41. pāpādīhi bhūmiyā|

pāpādīhī parā yā bhūmi tassā samāsantau a hauti| pāpabhūmaṃ, jātibhūmaṃ|

42. saṅkhyāhi|

saṅkhyāhi parā yā bhūmi tassā samāsantau a hauti| dvibhūmaṃ, tibhūmaṃ|

43. nadīgaudāvarīnaṃ|

saṅkhyāhi parāsaṃ nadīgaudāvarīnaṃ samāsantau a hauti, pañ‍canadaṃ, sattagaudāvaraṃ, saṅkhyāhitvaiva? mahānadī, nadīgaudāvarīnanti kiṃ? dasitthi|

44. asaṅkhyaihi cāṅgulyānañ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthaisu|

asaṅkhyaihi saṅkhyāhi ca parāya aṅgulyā samāsantau a hauti nau cai añ‍ñapadatthai asaṅkhyatthai ca samāsau vattatai| niggatamaṅgulīhi niraṅgulaṃ, ac‍caṅgulaṃ, dvai aṅguliyau samāhaṭā dvaṅgulaṃ, anañ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthaisūti kiṃ? pañ‍caṅguli hatthau, upaṅguli, kathaṃ  ‘dvai aṅgulīmānamassāti dvaṅgula’nti? nātra samāsauñ‍ñapadatthai vihitau mattādīnaṃ laupai katai tattha vattatai| aṅgulasaddau vā pamāṇavāci saddantaraṃ, yathā ‘sainaṅgulappamāṇaina aṅgulānaṃ sataṃ puṇṇaṃ catuddasa vā aṅgulānī’ti|

45. dīghāhauvassaikadaisaihi ca rattyā|

dīghādīhi asaṅkhyaihi saṅkhyāhi ca paramasmā rattiyā samāsantau a hauti| dīgharattaṃ, ahaurattaṃ (ttau), vassārattaṃ (ttau), pubbarattaṃ, apararattaṃ, aḍḍhurattaṃ, atik‍kantau rattiṃ atirattau, dvairattī samāhaṭā dirattaṃ (ttau), vā k‍vaci bahulādhikārā aikarattaṃ (ttau), aikaratti, anañ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthaisutvaiva? dīgharattihaimantau, uparatti, k‍vaci hautaiva bahulaṃ vidhānāyathārattaṃ|

46. gautvacatthai cālaupai|

gausaddā alaupavisayā samāsantau a hauti na cai catthai samāsau añ‍ñapadatthai asaṅkhyatthai ca, rājagavau, paramagavau, pañ‍cagavadhanau, dasagavaṃ, alaupaiti kiṃ? pañ‍cahi gauhi kītau pañ‍cagu, acatthaiti ki? ajassagāvau, anañ‍ñāsaṅkhyatthaisutvaiva? cittagu, upagu|

47. rattindivadāragavacaturassā|

aitai saddā aantā nipac‍cantai| rattau ca divā ca rattindivaṃ, ratti ca divā ca rattindivaṃ, dārā ca gāvau ca dāragavaṃ, catassau assiyau assa caturassau|

48. āyāmainugavaṃ|

anugavanti  nipac‍catai āyāmai gamyamānai| anugavaṃ sakaṭaṃ, āyāmaiti kiṃ? gun‍naṃ pañchā anugu|

49. akkhismāñ‍ñatthai| akkhismā samāsantau a hauti añ‍ñatthai cai samādhasā| visālakkhau, visālakkhī|

50. dārumyaṅgulyā|

aṅgulantā añ‍ñapadatthai dārumhi samāsantau a hauti| dvaṅgulaṃdāru, pañ‍caṅgulaṃ, aṅgulisadisāvayavaṃ dhañ‍ñādīnaṃ vikkhaipakaṃ dāruṃ vuc‍catai, pamāṇai tu pubbai viya siddhaṃ sakharājasaddā akārantāva, sissaupi na dissati, gāṇḍī vadhanvāti pakatantaraina siddhaṃ|

51. ci vītihārai|

aughābyatihārai gamyamānai añ‍ñapadatthai vattamānatau ci hauti| kaisākaisi daṇḍādaṇḍi, cakārau ‘‘cismi’’nti (3.66) visaisanatthau, sugandhi, duggantīti payaugau na dissatai|

52. ltvitthiyūhi kau|

ltuppac‍cayantaihi, itthiyamīkārūkārantaihi ca bahulaṃ kappac‍cayau hauti añ‍ñapadatthai| bahukattukau, bahukumārikau, bahubrahmabandhukau, bahulaṃtvaiva? subbhū|

53. vāñ‍ñatau|

añ‍ñaihi  añ‍ñapadatthai kau vā bahulaṃ hauti| bahumālakau, bahumālau|

54. uttarapadai| aitamadhikataṃ vaiditabbaṃ|

55. imassidaṃ|

uttarapadai paratau imassa idaṃ hauti| idamaṭṭhitā, idappac‍cayatā, niggahītalaupau passa ca dvibhāvau|

56. puṃ pumassa vā| pumassa puṃ haututtarapadai vibhāsā| pul‍liṅgaṃ, pumaliṅgaṃ|

57. ṭantantūnaṃ|

aisaṃ ṭa haututtarapadai k‍vaci vā| bhavampatiṭṭhāmayaṃ, bhagavaṃmūlakā nau dhammā, bahulādhikārā tarādīsu ca pagaiva mahattarī, rattañ‍ñumahattaṃ|

58. a| aisaṃ a haututtarapadai| guṇavantapatiṭṭhau-smi|

59. manādyāpādīnamau mayai ca| manādīnamāpādīnaṃ ca au haututtarapadai mayai ca| manausaiṭṭhā, manaumayā, rajaujal‍laṃ, rajaumayaṃ, āpaugataṃ, āpaumayaṃ, anuyanti disaudisaṃ|

60. parassa saṅkhyāsu|

saṅkhyāsuttarapadaisu  parassa au hauti| parausataṃ, parausahassaṃ, saṅkhyāsūti kiṃ? paradattūpajīvinau|

61. janai puthassu|

janai uttarapadai puthassa u hauti| ariyaihi puthagaivāyaṃ janauti puthuj‍janau|

62. sau chassāhāyatanai vā|

ahai āyatanai cuttarapadai chassa sau vā hauti| sāhaṃ chāhaṃ, saḷāyatanaṃ, chaḷāyatanaṃ|

63. ltupitādīnamāraṅaraṅa|

ltuppac‍cayantānaṃ pitādīnañ‍ca yathāk‍kamamāraṅaraṅa vā hauntuttarapadai, satthāradassanaṃ, kattāraniddaisau, mātarapitarau, vātvaiva? satthudassanaṃ, mātāpitarau|

64. vij‍jāyaunisambandhānamā tatra catthai|

ltupitādīnaṃ vij‍jāsambandhīnaṃ yaunisambandhīnaṃ ca taisvaiva ltupitādīsu vij‍jāyaunisambandhisuttarapadaisu catthavisayai ā hauti| hautāpautarau mātāpitarau, ltupitādīnaṃ tvaiva? puttabhātarau, tatraiti kiṃ? pitupitāmahā, catthaiti kiṃ? mātubhātā, vij‍jāyaunisambandhānanti kiṃ? dātubhattārau|

65. puttai|

puttai  uttarapadai catthavisayai ltupitādīnaṃ vij‍jāyauni sambandhānamā hauti| pitāputtā, mātāputtā|

66. cismiṃ|

cippac‍cayantai uttarapadai ā hauti| kaisākaisi, muṭṭhāmuṭṭhi|

67. itthiyambhāsitapumitthī pumaivaikatthai|

itthiyaṃ vattamānai aikatthai samānādhikaraṇai uttarapadai parai bhāsitapumā itthī pumaiva hauti| kumārabhariyau, dīghajaṅghau, yuvajāyau, itthiyanti kiṃ? kalyāṇī padhānamaisaṃ kalyāṇippadhānā, bhāsitapumaiti kiṃ? kañ‍ñābhariyau, itthīti kiṃ? gāmaṇikulaṃ diṭṭhi assa gāmaṇidiṭṭhi, aikatthaiti kiṃ? kalyāṇiyā mātā kalyāṇimātā|

68. k‍vaci pac‍cayai|

bhāsitapumitthī pac‍cayai k‍vaci pumaiva mauti| byattatarā, byattatamā|

69. sabbādayau vuttimattai|

itthivācakā sabbādayau vuttimattai pumaiva haunti| tassā mukhaṃ tammukhaṃ, tassaṃ tatra, tāya tatau, tassaṃ vailāyaṃ tadā|

70. jāyāya jayaṃ patimhi|

patimhi parai jāyāya jayaṃ hauti, jayampatī, ‘jānipatī’ti pakatantaraina siddhaṃ, tathā ‘dampatī, jampatī’ti|

71. sañ‍ñāyamudaudakassa|

sañ‍ñāyamudakassuttarapadai  udādaisau hauti| udadhi, udapānaṃ|

72. kumbhādīsu vā|

kumbhādīsuttarapadaisu udakassa udādaisau vā hauti| udakumbhau udakakumbhau, udapattau udakapattau, udabindhu udakabindhu, ākatigaṇau-yaṃ|

73. sautādīsūlaupau|

sautādīsuttarapadaisu udakassa ussa laupau hauti| dakasautaṃ, dakarakkhasau|

74. ṭa naña ssa|

uttarapadai naña saddassa ṭa hauti| abrāhmaṇau, ñakārau kiṃ? kaivalassa mā hautu pāmanaputtau|

75. ana sarai|

sarādau uttarapadai naña saddassa ana hauti| anakkhātaṃ|

76. nakhādayau|

nakhādayau saddā anana ṭādaisā nipac‍cantai| nāssa khamatthīti nakhau, akhamañ‍ñaṃ, sañ‍ñāsaddaisu ca nipphattimattaṃ yathākathañ‍ci kattabbaṃnāssa kulamathīti nakulau, akulamañ‍ñaṃ, nakha nakula napuṃsakanakkhatta nāka aivamādi|

77. nagau vāppāṇini|

nagaic‍cappāṇini  vā nipac‍catai| nagau rukkhau, nagau pabbatau, agau rukkhau, agau pabbatau, appāṇinīti kiṃ? agau vasalau sītaina|

78. sahassa sau-ñ‍ñatthai|

añ‍ñapadatthavutthimhi samāsai uttarapadai parai sahassa sau vā hauti| saputtau, sahaputtau, añ‍ñatthaiti kiṃ? saha katvā, saha yujjhitvā|

79. sañ‍ñāyaṃ|

sahassuttarapadai sau hauti sañ‍ñāyaṃ| sāssatthaṃ, sapalāsaṃ|

80. appac‍cakkhai|

appac‍cakkhai gamyamānai sahassa sau haututtarapadai, sāggi kapautau, sapisācā vātamaṇḍalikā|

81. akālai sakatthai|

sakatthappadhānassa sahasaddassa akālai uttarapadai sau hauti| sampan‍naṃ brahmaṃ sabrahmaṃ, sacak‍kaṃ nidhaihi, sadhuraṃ pājaihi, akālaiti kiṃ? saha pubbaṇhaṃ, sahāparaṇhaṃ|

82. ganthantādhikyai|

ganthantai  ādhikyai ca vattamānassa sahassa sau haututtarapadai| sakalaṃ jautimadhītai samuhuttaṃ, kālatthau ārambhau, ādhikyai-sadauṇā khārī, samāsakau kahāpaṇau, nic‍catthauyamārambhau|

83. samānassa pakkhādīsu vā|

pakkhādīsuttarapadaisu samānassa sau hauti vā| sapakkhau samānapakkhau, sajauti samānajauti, pakkhādīsūti kiṃ? samānasīlau, pakkha, jauti, janapada, ratti, pattinī, pattī, nābhi, bandhu brahmacārī, nāma, gautta, rūpa, ṭhāna, vaṇṇa, vayau, vacana, dhamma, jātiya, ghac‍ca|

84. udarai iyai|

udarai iyai parai paratau samānassa sau vā hauti| saudariyau, samānaudariyau, iyaiti kiṃ? samānaudaratā|

85. rīrikkhakaisu|

aitaisu samānassa sau hauti| sarī, sarikkhau, sarisau|

86. sabbādīnamā|

rīrikkhakaisu sabbādīnamā hauti| yādī, yādikkhau, yādisau|

87. ntakimimānaṃ ṭākīṭī|

rīrikkhakaisu  ntasadda kiṃsadda imasaddānaṃ ṭākīṭī haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ| bhavādī, bhavādikkhau, bhavādisau, kīdī, kīdikkhau, kīdisau, īdī, īdikkhau, īdisau|

88. tumhāmhānaṃ tāmaikasmiṃ|

rīrikkhakaisu tumhāmhānaṃ tāmā hauntaikasmiṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ| tādī, tādikkhau, tādisau, mādī, mādikkhau, mādisau| aikasminti kiṃ? tumhādī, amhādī, tumhādikkhau, amhādikkhau, tumhādisau, amhādisau|

89. taṃ mamañ‍ñatra|

rīrikkhakantatau añ‍ñasmiṃ uttarapadai tumhāmhānamaikasmiṃ taṃmaṃ haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ, tandīpā, mandīpā, taṃsaraṇā, maṃsaraṇā, tayyaugau, mayyaugauti bindulaupau|

90. vaitassaiṭa|

rīrikkhakaisvaitassaiṭa vā hauti, aidī, aitādī, aidikkhau, aitādikkhau, aidisau, aitādisau|

91. vidhādīsu dvissa du|

dvissa du hauti vidhādīsu, duvidhau, dupaṭṭaṃ aivamādi|

92. di guṇādīsu|

guṇādīsu dvissa di hauti, dviguṇaṃ, diratti, digu aivamādi|

93. tīsva|

tīsu  dvissa ahauti| dvattikkhattuṃ, dvittipattapurā|

94. ā saṅkhyāyāsatādau-nañ‍ñatthai|

saṅkhyāyamuttarapadai dvissa ā hauti asatādau anañ‍ñadhattha| dvādasa, dvāvīsati dvattiṃsa, saṅkhyāyanti kiṃ? dirattaṃ, asatādauti kiṃ? disataṃ, disahassaṃ| anañ‍ñatthaiti kiṃ? dvidasā|

95. tissai|

saṅkhyāyamuttarapadai tissa ai hauti asatādau anañ‍ñatthai, tairasa, taivīsa, taittiṃsa, saṅkhyāyaṃtvaiva? tirattaṃ, asatādautvaiva? tisataṃ, anañ‍ñatthaitvaiva? ticatukā|

96. cattālīsādau vā|

tissai vā hauti cattālīsādau, taicattālāsaṃ ticattālīsaṃ, taipañ‍ñāsaṃ tipañ‍ñāsaṃ, taisaṭṭhi tisaṭṭhi, taisattati tisattati, taiasīti tiyāsīti, tainavuti tinavuti, asatādautvaiva? tisataṃ|

97. dvissā ca|

asatādau-nañ‍ñatthai cattālīsādau dvissai vā hauti ā ca| dvaicattālīsaṃ, dvācattālīsaṃ dvicattālīsaṃ, dvaipañ‍ñāsaṃ, dvāpañ‍ñāsaṃ dvipañ‍ñāsaṃ ic‍cādi|

98. bācattālīsādau|

dvissa  bā vā hauti acattālīsādau-nañ‍ñatthai| bārasa dvādasa, bāvīsati dvāvīsati, battiṃsa dvattiṃsa, acattālīsādauti kiṃ? dvicattālīsaṃ|

99. vīsatidasaisu pañ‍cassa paṇṇapan‍nā|

vīsatidasaisu paraisu pañ‍cassa paṇṇapan‍nā haunti vā yathāk‍kamaṃ| paṇṇavīsati pañ‍cavīsati, pan‍narasva pañ‍cadasa|

100. catussa cucau dasai|

catussa cucau haunti vā dasasaddai parai| cuddasa, cauddasa, catuddasa|

101. chassa sau|

chassa sauic‍cayamādaisau hauti dasasaddai parai| sauḷasa|

102. aikaṭṭhānamā|

aikaaṭṭhānaṃ ā hauti dasai parai| aikādasa, aṭṭhārasa|

103. ra saṅkhyātau vā|

saṅkhyātau parassa dasassa ra hauti vibhāsā| aikārasa aikādasa, bārasa dvādasa, pan‍narasa pañ‍cadasa, sattarasa sattadasa, aṭṭhārasa aṭṭhādasa, pan‍nabādaisaisu nic‍caṃ, idha na hauti catuddasa|

104. chatīhi ḷau ca|

chatīhi  parassa dasassa ḷau hauti rau ca, sauḷasa saurasa, taiḷasa tairasa|

105. catuttha tatiyāna maḍḍhuḍḍhatiyā|

aḍḍhā paraisaṃ catutthatatiyānaṃ uḍḍhatiyā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ| aḍḍhaina catutthau aḍḍhuḍḍhau, aḍḍhaina tatiyau aḍḍhatiyau, kathaṃ aḍḍhataiyyauti? sakatthai ṇyai uttarapadavuḍḍhi|

106. dutiyassa saha diyaḍḍhadivaḍḍhā|

aḍḍhā parassa dutiyassa saha aḍḍhasaddaina diyaḍḍhadivaḍḍhā haunti| aḍḍhaina dutiyau diyaḍḍhau, divaḍḍhau vā|

107. sarai kada kussuttaratthai|

kussuttarapadatthai vattamānassa sarādau uttarapadai kadādaisau hauti| kadan‍naṃ, kadasanaṃ, saraiti kiṃ? kuputtau, uttaratthaiti kiṃ? kuauḍḍhau rājā|

108. kāppatthai|

appatthai vattamānassa kussa kā hauti uttarapadatthai, appakaṃ lavaṇaṃ kālavaṇaṃ|

109. purisai vā|

kussa purisai kā hauti vā| kāpurisau kupurisau, ayamappattavibhāsā, appatthai tu pubbaina nic‍caṃ hauti īsaṃ purisau kāpurisau|

110. pubbāparaj‍jasāyamajjhaihāhassa ṇhau|

pubbādīhuttarapadassa  ahassa ṇhādaisau hauti, pubbaṇhau, aparaṇhau, aj‍jaṇhau, sāyaṇhau, majjhaṇhau (paṇhau)|

iti mauggal‍lānai byākaraṇai vuttiyaṃ

samāsakaṇḍau tatiyau|

4. catutthau kaṇḍau (ṇādi)

1. ṇau vā pac‍cai|

chaṭṭhiyantā nāmasmā vā ṇappac‍cayau hauti apac‍cai-bhidhaiyyai, ṇakārau vuddhyatthau, aivamañ‍ñattāpi, vasiṭṭhassāpac‍caṃ vāsiṭṭhau, vāsiṭṭhī vā, aupagavau, aupagavī vā, vaiti vākyasamāsavikappanatthaṃ, tassādhikārau sakatthāvadhi|

2. vacchāditau ṇānaṇāyanā|

vacchādīhi apac‍cappac‍cayantaihi gauttādīhi ca saddaihi ṇānaṇāyanappac‍cayā vā haunti apac‍cai, vacchānau vacchāyanau, kac‍cānau kac‍cāyanau, yāgamai kātiyānau, mauggal‍lānau mauggal‍lāyanau, sākaṭānau sākaṭāyanau, kaṇhānau kaṇhāyanau ic‍cādi|

3. kattikāvidhavādīhi ṇaiyyaṇairā|

kattikādīhi vidhavādīhi ca ṇaiyyaṇairā vā yathāk‍kamaṃ haunti apac‍cai, kattikaiyyau, vainataiyyau, bhāginaiyyau ic‍cādi, vaidhavairau, bandhakairau, nālikairau, sāmaṇairau ic‍cādi|

4. ṇyadic‍cādīhi|

ditippabhutihi ṇyau vā hauti apac‍cai, daic‍cau, ādic‍cau, kauṇḍañ‍ñau, gaggyau, bhātabbau ic‍cādi|

5. ā ṇi|

akārantatau ṇi vā hauti apac‍cai, dakkhi, datthi, dauṇi| vāsavi, vāruṇi ic‍cādi|

6. rājatau ñ‍ñau jātiyaṃ|

rājasaddatau ñ‍ñau vā hauti apac‍cai jātiyaṃ gamyamānāyaṃ, rājañ‍ñau, jātiyanti kiṃ? rājāpac‍caṃ|

7. khattā yiyā|

khattasaddā yaiyā haunti apac‍cai jātiyaṃ, khatyau, khattiyau, jātiyaṃtvaiva? khatti|

8. manutau ssasaṇa|

manusaddatau jātiyaṃ ssasaṇa haunti apac‍cai, manussau, mānusau, itthiyaṃ manussā, mānusī, jātiyaṃtvaiva? mānavau|

9. janapadanāmasmā khattiyā rañ‍ñai ca ṇau|

janapadassa yaṃ nāmaṃ, tan‍nāmasmā khattiyā apac‍cai rañ‍ñai ca ṇau hauti, pañ‍cālau, kausalau, māgadhau, auk‍kākau, janapadanāmasmāti  kiṃ? dāsarathi, khattiyāti kiṃ? pañ‍cālassa brāhmaṇassa apac‍caṃ pañ‍cāli|

10. ṇya kurusivīhi|

kurusivīhi apac‍cai rañ‍ñai ca ṇyau hauti| kaurabyau, saibyau|

11. ṇa rāgā taina rattaṃ|

rāgavācitatiyantatau rattamicchaitasmiṃ atthai ṇau hauti, kasāvaina rattaṃ kāsāvaṃ, kausumbhaṃ, yāliddaṃ, rāgāti kiṃ? daivadattaina rattaṃ vatthaṃ, idha kasmā na hauti? ‘nīlaṃ pīta’nti, guṇavacanattā vināpi ṇaina ṇatthassābhidhānatau|

12. nakkhattaininduyuttaina kālai|

tatiyantatau nakkhattā taina lakkhitai kālai ṇau hauti, tañ‍cai nakkhattaminduyuttaṃ hauti, phussī ratti, phussaṃ ahaṃ, nakkhattainaiti kiṃ? gurunā lakkhitā ratti| induyuttainaiti kiṃ? katthikāya lakkhitau muhuttau, kālaiti kiṃ? phussaina lakkhitā atthasiddhi, aj‍jakattikāti kattikāyuttai candai kattikāsaddau vattatai|

13. sā-ssa daivatā puṇṇamāsī|

saiti paṭhamantā assāti chaṭṭhyatthai ṇau bhavati, yaṃ paṭhamantaṃ, sā cai daivatā puṇṇamāsīvā, sugatau daivatā assābhi  saugatau, māhindau, yāmau, vāruṇau, phussī puṇṇamāsī assa sambandhinīti phussau, māsau, māyau, phaggunau, cittau, vaisākhau| jaiṭṭhamūlau, āsaḷhau, sāvaṇau, pauṭṭhapādau, assayujau, kattikau, māgasirau, puṇṇamāsīti kiṃ? phussī pañ‍camī assa, puṇṇamāsī ca bhatakamāsasambandhinī na hauti… puṇṇau mā assanti nibbacanā, atau aiva nipātanā ṇau sāgamau ca, māsasutiyāva na pañ‍cadasa rattādau vidhi|

14. tamadhītai taṃ jānāti kaṇikā ca|

dutiyantatau tamadhītai taṃ jānātīti aitaisvatthaisu ṇau hauti ṇau ṇikau ca, byākaraṇamadhītai jānāti vā vaiyyākaraṇau, chandasau, kamakau, padakau, vainayikau, suttantikau, dvitaggahaṇaṃ puthagaiva vidhānatthaṃ jānanassa ca ajjhainavisayabhāvadassanatthaṃ pasiddhūpasaṅgahatthaṃ ca|

15. tassa visayai daisai|

chaṭṭhiyantā visayai daisarūpai ṇau hauti, vasātīnaṃ visayau daisau vāsātau, daisaiti kiṃ? cakkhussa visayau rūpaṃ, daivadattassa visayau-nuvākau|

16. nivāsai tan‍nāmai|

chaṭṭhiyantā nivāsai daisai tan‍nāmai ṇau hauti, sivīnaṃ nivāsau daisau saibyau, vāsātau|

17. adūrabhavai|

chaṭṭhiyantā  adūrabhavai daisai tan‍nāmai ṇau hauti, vidisāya adūrabhavaṃ vaidisaṃ|

18. taina nibbattai|

tatiyantā nibbattai daisai tan‍nāmai ṇau hauti, kusambaina nibbattā kausambhī nagarī, kākandī, mākandī, sahassaina nibbattā sāhassī parīkhā, hautumhi kattari karaṇai ca yathāyaugaṃ tatiyā|

19. tamīdhatthi|

tanti paṭhamantā idhāti sattamyatthai daisai tan‍nāmai ṇau hauti, yantaṃ paṭhamantamatthi cai, udumbarā asmiṃ daisai santīti audumbarau, bādarau, babbajau|

20. tatra bhavai|

sattamyantā bhavatthai ṇau hauti, udakai bhavau audakau, aurasau, jānapadau, māgadhau, kāpilavatthavau, kausambau|

21. aj‍jādīhi tanau|

bhavatthai aj‍jādīhi tanau hauti, aj‍ja bhavau aj‍jatanau, svātanau, hiyyattanau|

22. purātau ṇau ca|

purāic‍casmā bhavatthai ṇau hauti tanau ca, purāṇau, purātanau|

23. amātvac‍cau|

amāsaddatau  ac‍cau hauti bhavatthai, amac‍cau|

24. majjhāditvimau|

majjhādīhi sattamyantaihi bhavattai imau hauti, majjhimau, antimau| majjha, anta, haiṭṭhā, upari, aura, pāra, pacchā, abbhantara, pac‍canta (puratthā, bāhira)|

25. kaṇaṇaiyyaṇaiyyakayiyā|

sattamyantā aitai pac‍cayā haunti bhavatthai, kaṇa-kusinā rāyaṃ bhavau kausinārakau, māgadhakau, ārañ‍ñakau vihārau| ṇaiyya-gaṅgaiyyau, pabbataiyyau, vānaiyyau| ṇaiyyaka-kaulaiyyakau, bārāṇasaiyyakau, campaiyyakau, mithilaiyyakauti aiyyakau| ya-gammau, dibbau| iyagāmiyau, udari-yau, diviyau, pañ‍cāliyau, baudhipakkhiyau, laukiyau|

26. ṇikau|

sattamyantā bhavatthai ṇikau hauti, sāradikau divasau, sāradikā ratti|

27. tamassa sippaṃ sīlaṃ paṇyaṃ paharaṇaṃ payaujanaṃ|

paṭhamantā sippādivācakā assaitichaṭṭhiyatthaiṇikau hauti, vīṇāvādanaṃ sippamassa vaiṇikau, maudaṅgikau, vaṃsikau, paṃsukū- ladhāraṇaṃ  sīlamassa paṃsukūlikau, taicīvarikaiva, gandhaupaṇyamassa gandhikau, tailikau, gauḷikau, cāpau paharaṇamassa cāpikau, taumarikau, muggarikau, upadhippayaujanamassa aupadhikaṃ, sātikaṃ, sāhassikaṃ|

28. taṃ hantarahati gacchatuñchati carati|

dutiyantā hantīti aivamādīsvatthaisu ṇīkau hauti| pakkhīhipakkhinau hantīti pakkhikau, sākunikau, māyūrikau| macchaihi-macchikau, mainikau| migaihi-māgavikau hāriṇikau, ‘sūkarikau’ti ikau| satamarahatīti sātikaṃ, sandiṭṭhikau, aihipassavidhiṃ arahatīti aihipassikau, sāhassikau, ‘sahassiyau’ti iyau| paradāraṃ gacchatīti pāradārikau, maggikau, pañ‍ñāsayaujanikau| badarai uñchatīti bādarikau, sāmākikau| dhammaṃ caratīti dhammikau, adhammikau|

29. taina kataṃ kītaṃ baddhamabhisaṅkhataṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ hataṃ hanti jitaṃ jayati dibbati khaṇati tarati carati vahati jīvati|

tatiyantā katādisvatthaisu ṇikau hauti| kāyaina kataṃ kāyikaṃ, vācasikaṃ, mānasikaṃ, vātaina katau ābādhau vātikau| sataina kītaṃ sātikaṃ, sāhassikaṃ, mūlatauva, daivadataina kītanti na hauti tadatthāppatītiyā| varattāya baddhau vārattikau, āyasikau, pāsikau| ghataina abhisaṅkhataṃ saṃsaṭṭhaṃ vā ghātikaṃ, gauḷikaṃ, dādhikaṃ, māricikaṃ| jālaina hatau hantīti vā jālikau, bālīsikau| akkhaihi jitamakkhikaṃ, sālākikaṃ| akkhaihi jayati dibbatīti vā akkhikau| khaṇittiyā  khaṇatīti khāṇittikau, kuddālikau, daivadattaina jitaṃ, aṅgulyā khaṇatīti na hauti tadatthānavagamā| uḷumpaina taratīti auḷumpikau, ‘uḷumpikau’ti ikau gaupucchikau, nāvikau| sakaṭaina caratīti sākaṭikau, ‘rathikau, parappikau’ti ikau| khandhaina vahatīti khandhikau, aṃsikau, ‘sīsikau’ti ikau| vaitanaina jīvatīti vaitanikau, ‘bhatikau, kayikau, vik‍kayikau, kayavik‍kayikau’ti ikau|

30. tassa saṃvattati|

catutthiyantā saṃvattatīti asmiṃ atthai ṇikau hauti, punabbhavāya saṃvattatīti paunaubhavikau, itthiyaṃ paunau bhavikā, laukāya saṃvattatīti laukikau suṭṭhu aggauti saggau, saggāya saṃvattatīti sauvaggikau, sassauvaka tadaminādipāṭhā, dhanāya saṃvattatīti dhañ‍ñaṃ, yau|

31. tatau sambhūtamāgataṃ|

pañ‍camyantā sambhūtamāgatanti aitaisvatthaisu ṇikau hauti, mātikau sambhūtamāgataṃ vā mattikaṃ, paittikaṃ, ṇyariyaṇa -yāpi dissanti, surabhitau sambhūtaṃ saurabhyaṃ, thanatau sambhūtaṃ thañ‍ñaṃ, pititau sambhūtau paittiyau, mātiyau, mattiyau, mac‍cau vā|

32. tattha vasati viditau bhattau niyuttau|

sattamyantā tattha vasatītvaivamādīsvatthaisu ṇikau hauti| rukkhamūlai vasatīti rukkhamūlikau, ārañ‍ñikau, sausānikau| laukai viditau laukikau| catumahārājaisu bhattā cātummahārājikā| dvārai niyuttau dauvārikau dassauka tadaminādipāṭhā, bhaṇḍāgārikau, ikau-navakammikau, kiyaujātikiyau, andhakiyau|

33. tassidaṃ|

chaṭṭhiyantā idamic‍casmiṃ atthai ṇikau hauti, saṅghassa idaṃ saṅghikaṃ, puggalikaṃ, sakyaputtikau, nātiputtikau, jainadattikau, kiyai-sakiyau, parakiyau, niyai-attaniyaṃ, kai- sakau rājakaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ|

34. ṇau|

chaṭṭhiyantā idamic‍casmiṃ atthai ṇau hauti, kac‍cāyanassa idaṃ kac‍cāyanaṃ byākaraṇaṃ, saugataṃ sāsanaṃ, māhisaṃ maṃsādi|

35. gavādīhi yau|

gavādīhi chaṭṭhiyantaihi idamic‍casmiṃ atthai yau hauti, gun‍naṃ idaṃ gabyaṃ maṃsādi, kabyaṃ, dabbaṃ|

36. pititau bhātari raiyyaṇa|

pitusaddā tassa bhātari raiyyaṇa hauti, pitu bhātā paittaiyyau|

37. mātitau ca bhaginiyaṃ chau|

mātitau pititau ca taisaṃ bhaginiyaṃ chau hauti, mātu bhaginī mātucchā, pitu bhaginī pitucchā, kathaṃ ‘mātulau’ti ‘‘mātulāditvānī’’ti nipātanā|

38. mātāpitūsvāmahau|

mātāpitūhi  taisaṃ mātāpitūsu āmahau hauti, mātu mātā mātāmahī, mātu pitā mātāmahau, pitu mātā pitāmahī, pitu pitā pitāmahau, na yathāsaṅkhyaṃ, pac‍caikābhi sambandhā|

39. hitai raiyyaṇa|

mātāpitūhi hitai raiyyaṇa hauti, mattaiyyau, paittaiyyau|

40. nindāñ‍ñātappapaṭibhāgarassadayāsañ‍ñāsu kau|
